# Indexserver Data Types

**Version:** 25.0.1.5

Generated by `scripts/generate-api-docs.py` from `openapi.json`. Do not edit.
For task-oriented guidance, read `skills/elo-indexserver-rest/SKILL.md` and `knowledge/` first.

Model schemas: 841. Request (BRequest_*: 414) and result (BResult_*: 141) wrapper schemas are listed per operation in `operations.md`.

Classes ending in `C` hold constants (read them at runtime via `getConstants`).
Classes ending in `Z` are element selectors that control which members are read or written.

## `AccessC`

This class defines constants for access rights. The `FLAG_*` constants are used in `UserInfo#flags`. The `LUR_*` constants are used in access control lists, `AclItem#access`.

| Property | Type | Description |
|----------|------|-------------|
| `FLAG2_ALL` | int32 | User right: all rights for `UserInfo#flags2`. Default flags2 value for administrators. |
| `FLAG2_ANALYTICS_DASHBOARD_EDIT` | int32 | User right: analytics dashboard edit. This right allows a user to access the 'dashboard' area at ELO Analytics and to edit dashboards. |
| `FLAG2_ANALYTICS_DASHBOARD_VIEW` | int32 | User right: analytics dashboard view. This right allows a user to view predefined dashboards. |
| `FLAG2_ANALYTICS_DISCOVER` | int32 | User right: analytics discover. This right allows a user to access the 'discover' area at ELO Analytics and to edit searches. |
| `FLAG2_ANALYTICS_VISUALIZE` | int32 | User right: analytics visualize. This right allows a user to access the 'visualize' area at ELO Analytics and to edit visualizations. |
| `FLAG2_CAN_BE_SUBSTITUTED` | int32 | Group right: This group can be substituted to an other user. This right cannot be inherited by sub groups and is not inherited by substituted groups or users. |
| `FLAG2_CAN_USE_AI` | int32 | User's right to use AI functions. The Indexserver only grants this right but does not enforce it. It is up to the AI assistant component to verify whether the user has this right. |
| `FLAG2_DESKTOP_CLIENT_PLUS` | int32 | Allows to use Desktop Client Plus. This value has to be used in the UserInfo.flags2 member. |
| `FLAG2_EDITFLOWS` | int32 | User right to edit data in the ELO Flows component. The Indexserver only provides this right but does not enforce it. It is up to the ELO Flows component to check for this user right. |
| `FLAG2_EXTEND_WORKFLOW_RIGHTS` | int32 | User right: extend ACL during workflow execution. This value has to be used in the UserInfo.flags2 member. |
| `FLAG2_FUNCTIONAL_ROLE` | int32 | Functional role that can be chosen by the user. |
| `FLAG2_INTERACTIVE_LOGIN` | int32 | User right: Allow interactive login. This value has to be used in the UserInfo.flags2 member. |
| `FLAG2_IS_DMS_DESKTOP_USER` | int32 | User right: user works with ELO DMS Desktop. This value has to be used in the UserInfo. flags2 member. |
| `FLAG2_LAST` | int32 | Highest bit currently used in `UserInfo#flags2`. Reserved. |
| `FLAG2_LIMITED_CLIENT` | int32 | User right: limited client. This right limits the functionality of the client application. In AdminConsole, this flag ist named "ELOxc Client User, Email only". |
| `FLAG2_SHOW_EXTRA_INFO` | int32 | User right: user can see additional keywording information. This value has to be used in the UserInfo.flags2 member. |
| `FLAG2_VISIBLE_FEED_USER` | int32 | User right: user is visible in document feed. This value has to be used in the UserInfo. flags2 member. |
| `FLAG2_VISIBLE_USER` | int32 | User right: user is visible in a select box. This value has to be used in the UserInfo. flags2 member. |
| `FLAG2_WF_CONTROLLER` | int32 | User right: workflow controller. This right allows a user to read all active workflows - even those where she or he is not involved. In IX 8.x, this functionality was implemented by the IX option … |
| `FLAGS2_NOT_TO_INHERIT` | int32 | Rights (UserInfo.flags2) which are not inherited from groups the user is a member. |
| `FLAGS2_NOT_TO_SUBSTITUTE` | int32 | Rights (UserInfo.flags2) which are not substituted. EIX-864, RID11413 |
| `FLAGS_EXCLUSIVE_FOR_GROUPS` | int32 | These rights are removed from UserInfo.flags if UserInfo.type == UserInfoC. TYPE_USER |
| `FLAGS_NOT_TO_INHERIT` | int32 | Rights (UserInfo.flags) which are not inherited from groups the user is a member. |
| `FLAGS_NOT_TO_SUBSTITUTE` | int32 | Rights (UserInfo.flags) which are not substituted. EIX-864 |
| `FLAG_ADMIN` | int32 | Main administrator, can edit all users and groups. |
| `FLAG_ALL` | int32 | All rights (without flag `FLAG_NOLOGIN` and `FLAG_IS_OPTION_GROUP` ). |
| `FLAG_AUTHOR` | int32 | Author for release documents |
| `FLAG_CHANGEMASK` | int32 | Change the storage mask of an existing (not new) archive entry. |
| `FLAG_CHANGEPATH` | int32 | Change storage path settings. |
| `FLAG_CHANGEPW` | int32 | Change password. |
| `FLAG_CHANGEREV` | int32 | Change the archiving mode of a document. |
| `FLAG_DELDOC` | int32 | Delete documents. |
| `FLAG_DELREADONLY` | int32 | Delete read only documents. |
| `FLAG_DELSTRUC` | int32 | Delete archive entries. |
| `FLAG_DELVERSION` | int32 | Delete a document version in history |
| `FLAG_EDITACL` | int32 | Edit security settings. |
| `FLAG_EDITACT` | int32 | Edit activity projects. |
| `FLAG_EDITCONFIG` | int32 | Edit master data |
| `FLAG_EDITDOCS` | int32 | Edit documents: checkin, checkout. |
| `FLAG_EDITDUEDATE` | int32 | Edit expiration date of archive entries. |
| `FLAG_EDITMASK` | int32 | Create, edit, delete storage masks. |
| `FLAG_EDITREPL` | int32 | Edit replication set assignment. |
| `FLAG_EDITSCAN` | int32 | Edit scanner settings. |
| `FLAG_EDITSCRIPT` | int32 | Create, edit, delete scripts. |

| `FLAG_EDITSWL` | int32 | Edit catchwords. |
| `FLAG_EDITWF` | int32 | Edit work flows. |

| `FLAG_IGNOREACL` | int32 | Ignore access control lists. |

| `FLAG_NOLOGIN` | int32 | User cannot login. This is not a right but a prohibition. |
| `FLAG_SAPADMIN` | int32 | SAP administrator. |
| `FLAG_STARTWF` | int32 | Start work flows. |
| `FLAG_SUBADMIN` | int32 | Administrator, can edit only the users and groups he created. |
| `LUR_ALL` | int32 | All access control rights. |
| `LUR_DELETE` | int32 | Access control right for deleting an archive entry. |
| `LUR_EDIT` | int32 | Access control right for beeing able to checkin a new document version. |
| `LUR_LIST` | int32 | Insert or remove an entry to the list of subentries of a Sord. |
| `LUR_NOTHING` | int32 | No access. |
| `LUR_PERMISSION` | int32 | Access control right to change ACL of an object. |
| `LUR_READ` | int32 | Access control right for reading an archive entry. |
| `LUR_SPACE` | int32 | Access control right to combine rights for an object contained in a workspace. |
| `LUR_WRITE` | int32 | Access control right for writing an archive entry. |

## `Action`

This class describes an entry in a document feed. There are three kinds of entries/actions in general. First, an action can be a comment added manually by a user. Second, scripts or programs can insert actions e.g. to notify about a particular state. Third, actions are generated by the system e.g. when a new document version is created. In order to add an action to a feed, call `FeedService#checkinAction(de.elo.ix.client.ClientInfo, Action, ActionZ)`. User comments can have a parent action to support a two level hierarchy of entries.

| Property | Type | Description |
|----------|------|-------------|
| `TStamp` | string | Time stamp. Time stamp of creation or modification. |
| `TStampSync` | string | Timestamp of this object's last export by the replication. |
| `acl` | string | Raw ACL representation. Only valid for Actions of type `EActionType#UserComment` or `EActionType#AutoComment`. This member is ignored if `Action#aclItems` is not null. |
| `aclItems` | AclItem[] |  |
| `changeCounter` | int32 | Counts the number of updates. This element is only valid for `EActionType#UserComment`. It counts the number of updates made to the comment. |
| `createDateIso` | string | Create date. This element is the ISO formatted create date of the action. When this object is received from `FeedService#checkoutAction(de.elo.ix.client.ClientInfo, String, ActionZ)` or … |
| `docVersion` | DocVersion | Document version object. This element is only valid for generated actions that belong to a document version, e.g. `EActionType#VersionCreated`. Read-only. |
| `docVersionGuid` | string | GUID of the associated document version. This element is only valid for generated actions that belong to a document version, e.g. `EActionType#VersionCreated`. Read-only. |
| `feedGuid` | string | Feed GUID. This action belongs to the feed identified by this GUID. |
| `guid` | string | Action GUID. Unique identifier. |
| `history` | ListOfActionHistory | Change history of text member. This list does not contain the current text. This member is null, if the text has not been changed. |
| `parentGuid` | string | Parent action GUID. This element is only valid for user comments, `EActionType#UserComment`. If not empty, this action is subordinated under the action given by this GUID. |
| `text` | string | Comment text. This element is only valid for `EActionType#UserComment`, and `EActionType#AutoComment`. |
| `type` | EActionType | Action type. |
| `updateDateIso` | string | Update date. This element is only valid for `EActionType#UserComment`. It holds the ISO formatted update date. When this object is received from … |
| `userGuid` | string | User GUID. The GUID of the user who has created this action. For actions of type `EActionType#SordCreated`, this member holds the GUID of Sord.ownerId. For actions of type … |
| `userId` | int32 | User ID. It is set to the numerical ID of the user given by `#userGuid`. This element is read-only. |
| `userName` | string | User name. It is set to the name of the user given by `#userGuid`. This element is read-only. |
| `workflow` | WFDiagram | Workflow object. This element is only valid for generated actions that belong to a workflow. Read-only. |
| `workflowGuid` | string | GUID of the associated workflow. This element is valid for action types that belong to a workflow. |

## `Activity`

This class represents an activity. An activity is a task delegated to an instance outside the ELO system. It is created when the task is sent to the instance and deleted, if it is received back. An activity defines a date for expecting the response, `dueDateIso`. At this date, the activity appears in the task list of the initiator. If the task is finished, the initiator sets the `backAt` member and the activity is closed. Activities can be used to observe a document or a folder. For each modification, a ELO_NOTIFY activity is created and displayed in the task list of the user that wants to observe the object. An activity object is an instance on an activity project. The project defines the properties the user can edit or select to provide more information to the task.

| Property | Type | Description |
|----------|------|-------------|
| `TStamp` | string | Date and time of the last update. Readonly |
| `TStampSync` | string | Timestamp of this object's last export by the replication. |
| `backAt` | string | Date when activity object was returned, ISO format. This value does not contain a time portion. |
| `backMode` | string | Application defined purpose. |
| `comment` | string | Comment for the activity. |
| `destination` | string | Application defined purpose. |
| `dueDateIso` | string | Date when activity object is expected to be returned. This value does not contain a time portion. |
| `fileName` | string | Application defined purpose. |
| `guid` | string | GUID of the activity object. |
| `name` | string | Short name/description of the activity object. |
| `objEntryType` | SordTypeEnum | Sord type of the associated folder or document. |
| `objGuid` | string | GUID of the assigned archive entry. |
| `objId` | int32 | Object ID of the associated folder or document. Read-only. |
| `objType` | int32 | Object type of the associated folder or document. Read-only. |
| `prio` | int32 | Priority for the activity. Priority A has value 0, UserTaskPriorityC. HIGHEST Priority B has value 1. Priority C has value 2, UserTaskPriorityC#LOWEST |
| `project` | string | Project name for the activity. |
| `receiverDisplayName` | string | Locale specific name of this reminder's receiver. Read-only. This value is the resolved `UserInfo#nameTranslationKey`. |
| `receiverId` | int32 | The ID of the recipient. |
| `receiverName` | string | Activity was created for this user. Readonly. |
| `revVers` | string | Revision version of the activity object. |
| `senderDisplayName` | string | Locale specific name of this reminder's sender. Read-only. This value is the resolved `UserInfo#nameTranslationKey`. |
| `senderId` | int32 | ID of the user who created the activity. |
| `senderName` | string | Activity was created by this user. Readonly. |
| `sentAtIso` | string | Date the activity was sent in ISO format. This value does not contain a time portion. |
| `sentMode` | string | Application defined purpose. |
| `transId` | string | Application defined value. |
| `ud0` | string | First application defined value. |
| `ud1` | string | Second application defined value. |
| `ud2` | string | Third application defined value. |
| `ud3` | string | Fourth application defined value. |
| `ud4` | string | Fifth application defined value. |
| `ud5` | string | Sixth application defined value. |
| `ud6` | string | Seventh application defined value. |
| `ud7` | string | Eighth application defined value. |
| `ud8` | string | Ninth application defined value. |
| `ud9` | string | Tenth application defined value. |

## `ActivityOption`

This class represents an option for an activity project.

| Property | Type | Description |
|----------|------|-------------|
| `id` | int32 | Option ID. This value can be one of the predefined option IDs in ActivityOptionC. Or you can use an application defined value greater or equal to ActivityOptionC.ID_VALUE. |
| `keywords` | string[] |  |
| `mandatory` | boolean | A value must be defined for this option. This option has to be checked by the client application. It is not checked by Indexserver. |
| `name` | string | Activity option name. The name must not contain one of this characters: ! $ - + * |
| `onlyKeyword` | boolean | The value can only be selected from the associated keyword list. This option has to be checked by the client application. It is not checked by Indexserver. |
| `readOnly` | boolean | The value can not be edited in the user interface. This option has to be checked by the client application. It is not checked by Indexserver. |
| `sorted` | boolean | The value has to be displayed in an appropriate order. This option has to be checked by the client application. It is not checked by Indexserver. |
| `stamp` | boolean | This option is a stamp field. This option has to be checked by the client application. It is not checked by Indexserver. |

## `ActivityOptionC`

Constants for class ActivityOption

| Property | Type | Description |
|----------|------|-------------|
| `ID_RECEIVER` | int32 | Option ID for receiver. |
| `ID_STATUS` | int32 | Option ID for receiving status. |
| `ID_TRANS` | int32 | Option ID for transmission number. |
| `ID_TYPE` | int32 | Option ID for shipping type. |
| `ID_VALUE` | int32 | Application defined IDs must be greater or equal to this value. Up to 10 application defined IDs can be defined. Their values have to be in the range from ID_VALUE+0 to ID_VALUE+9. |

This class represents an activity project. An activity project is a template for an activity.

## `AggregationOptions`

Use this class if aggregations in general (i.e. not context terms) should be retrieved by `de.elo.ix.client.IXServicePortIF#findAggregations(de.elo.ix.client.ClientInfo, de.elo.ix.client.FindInfo)`. Submit a map of AggregationQuerys to retrieve result aggregations for each of them. The resulting list `ContextTermResults#contextTerms` is provided in the same order as the submitted list contextTermQueries

Extends `ESearchOptions`.

| Property | Type | Description |
|----------|------|-------------|
| `aggregationQueries` | MapToAggregationQuery | Map of aggregation queries. |

## `AggregationOrderE`

Sort options for ElasticSearch. By default, the terms aggregation orders terms by descending document _count. The date_histogram aggregation, however, sorts buckets by default by their key ascending. Use the order parameter to specify a different sort order. We do not offer some sort order types if it is not guaranteed to produce reasonable results. E.g. Due to the way the terms aggregation gets terms from shards, sorting by ascending doc count often produces inaccurate results.

Values: `DEFAULT`, `COUNT_DESC`, `KEY_DESC`, `KEY_ASC`

## `AggregationQuery`

| Property | Type | Description |
|----------|------|-------------|
| `aggregationQueryParams` | AggregationQueryParams | Contains type specific parameters (e.g. for type=TERMS parameters size, sub-aggregations, ... ) |
| `fieldType` | FieldTypeE | Defines the concrete ES field together with searchField. Options are tokenized, not tokenized, numeric, date. |
| `indexFieldKey` | string | If searchfield equals to SearchFieldE.INDEXFIELD, provide the name of the index field here. If searchField != SearchFieldE.INDEXFIELD, this value is ignored. If the associated docmask is of type … |
| `name` | string |  |
| `searchField` | SearchFieldE | Provides the constant of the field to be aggregated. To aggregate an index field, set this to SearchFieldE.INDEXFIELD and provide its name in indexFieldKey. |
| `type` | AggregationTypeE |  |

## `AggregationQueryParams`

Base class for aggregation type specific parameters given in an AggregationQuery.

## `AggregationResult`

Return object for AggregationQueries. Consists of name and type and a type specific part. This type specific part contains actual results according to its AggregationType. Hence, in each instance of AggregationResult is only one type specific part valid. All other parts are not valid and equal to null.

| Property | Type | Description |
|----------|------|-------------|
| `dateHistogramResult` | DateHistogramAggregationResult | Type specific part: Result of an aggregation of type DATE_HISTOGRAM. |
| `filterResult` | FilterAggregationResult | Type specific part: Result of an aggregation of type FILTER. |
| `histogramResult` | HistogramAggregationResult | Type specific part: Result of an aggregation of type HISTOGRAM. |
| `name` | string | Name of the aggregation. |
| `rangeResult` | RangeAggregationResult | Type specific part: Result of an aggregation of type RANGE. |
| `singleValueMetricsResult` | SingleValueMetricsAggregationResult | Type specific part: Result of an aggregation of a single-value-metrics type (MIN, MAX, SUM, AVG). |
| `termsResult` | TermsAggregationResult | Type specific part: Result of an aggregation of type TERMS. |
| `type` | AggregationTypeE | Type of aggregation. |
| `valueCountResult` | ValueCountAggregationResult | Type specific part: Result of an aggregation of type VALUE_COUNT. |

## `AggregationTypeE`

Types of Aggregations for ESearch AggregationQuery.

Values: `TERMS`, `FILTER`, `MIN`, `MAX`, `SUM`, `AVG`, `VALUE_COUNT`, `HISTOGRAM`, `RANGE`, `DATE_HISTOGRAM`

## `AndOperator`

This operator contains a list of QueryObjects and concatenates them with an "AND" operation. This means, a document is returned if all of the QueryObjects are present in the document. For REST calls, use class QueryOperator and set the operator type in `QueryOperator#operatorType`.

Extends `QueryOperator`.

| Property | Type | Description |
|----------|------|-------------|
| `queryObjectList` | ListOfQueryObject | List of QueryObjects which are and combined. |

| `keywordListId` | string | Keyword List ID. |

| `TYPE_INTEGER` | int32 | Aspect line contains a number value without fraction in the range of (-2^31) to (2^31)-1. |
| `TYPE_ISO_DATE_ONLY` | int32 | Aspect line contains a date in ISO format without time digits. |
| `TYPE_ISO_DATE_TIME` | int32 | Aspect line contains a date in ISO format with time digits. |

## `BeginForwardWorkflowNodeInfo`

## `Bitset`

This class is used in the scripting API of Indexserver and provides bit operations on 64 bit integers. JavaScript does not support bit operations for 64 bit integers. The global context of the JavaScripts executed by Indexserver contain an object named Bitset which is an instance of this class.

| Property | Type | Description |
|----------|------|-------------|
| `v` | string | Internal 64 bit integer value |

## `Bucket`

Represents a bucket within an AggregationResult for a bucket aggregation.

| Property | Type | Description |
|----------|------|-------------|
| `aggregations` | MapToAggregationResult | In case the bucket aggregation query has defined sub-aggregations, this Map contains the results of that sub-aggregations within this bucket. |
| `docCount` | string | Number of documents belonging to the bucket's criteria. |
| `from` | string | For aggregations of type RANGE and DATE_RANGE, this value is a string representation of the lower bound of this bucket. |
| `key` | string | The bucket key identifying the bucket. |
| `to` | string | For aggregations of type RANGE and DATE_RANGE, this value is a string representation of the upper bound of this bucket. |
| `type` | AggregationTypeE | Type of aggregation. |

## `BucketKeyDateFormatE`

Date formats of the bucket key in return buckets of aggregations of type DATE_HISTOGRAM and DATE_RANGE.

Values: `ELO_ISO_DATE_FORMAT_MILLIS`, `ELO_ISO_DATE_FORMAT_SECONDS`, `ELO_ISO_DATE_FORMAT_DAY`, `ELO_ISO_DATE_FORMAT_YEAR`, `ELO_TSTAMP_FORMAT`

## `CalendarIntervalE`

Types of calendar intervals used for AggregationQuery of type DATE_HISTOGRAM. Note: The value NONE exists only to check if this mandatory parameter is given, since API serialization may deliver always the first enum value.

Values: `NONE`, `MINUTE`, `HOUR`, `DAY`, `WEEK`, `MONTH`, `QUARTER`, `YEAR`

## `Cardinality`

Aspect cardinality.

Values: `OPTIONAL`, `MANDATORY`, `MANDATORY_MANY`, `OPTIONAL_MANY`

## `ChangePasswordInfo`

Parameter object to change a user's password. All passwords in this object may (and should) be encrypted for enhanced security. To encrypt the fields, use `IXConnection#encryptPassword(String)`.

| Property | Type | Description |
|----------|------|-------------|
| `currentPassword` | string | Current password. Must be set and match the actual current password. |
| `newPassword` | string | New password to set. Must be different to the current password. |

## `CheckoutSordTypesInfo`

Use this object to define which sord types should be returned by `IXServicePortIF#checkoutSordTypes2`. This class contains several lists you can use to specify which SordTypes you want to get. The elements in one list are always searched with an OR operation. If various list are provided, they are connected using OR operator. If all lists are empty, all sord types are returned.

| Property | Type | Description |
|----------|------|-------------|
| `sordTypeGuids` | ListOfString | GUIDs of the sord types to checkout. |
| `sordTypeIds` | ListOfInteger | IDs of the sord types to checkout. |
| `sordTypeNames` | ListOfString | Names of the sord types to checkout. |

## `CheckoutSubstitutionsInfo`

This object is used to define which substitutions should be returned by `IXServicePortIF#checkoutSubstitutions`. The elements in one list are always search with an OR operation. If various list are provided, they are connected using OR (default) (set `#andOperator` to true to use AND operator)

| Property | Type | Description |
|----------|------|-------------|
| `andOperator` | boolean | Search for elements of different lists with OR operator (default) or AND (set value to true) |
| `creatorIds` | ListOfInteger | Returns all substitutions this users created. (This user is set as `Substitution#creatorId`) |
| `creatorNames` | ListOfString | Returns all substitutions this users created. (This user is set as `Substitution#creatorId`) |
| `includeSubstitutesGroups` | boolean | Include substitutions where `Substitution#substituteId` is set to the provided substitutes or any of their groups. |
| `substituteIds` | ListOfInteger | Returns all substitutions this users substitutes. (This user is set as `Substitution#substituteId`) |
| `substituteNames` | ListOfString | Returns all substitutions this users substitutes. (This user is set as `Substitution#substituteId`) |
| `substitutionGuids` | ListOfString | GUID of the substitutions to checkout. |
| `userIds` | ListOfInteger | Returns all substitutions of this users substitutes where this user is set as `Substitution#userId`. |
| `userNames` | ListOfString | Returns all substitutions of this users where this user is set as `Substitution#userId`. |

## `ContextTermOptions`

Use this class if context terms should be retrieved by `de.elo.ix.client.IXServicePortIF#findContextTerms(de.elo.ix.client.ClientInfo, de.elo.ix.client.FindInfo, int)`. Query is submitted by `de.elo.ix.client.esearch.ESearchParams`. Submit a list of ContextTermQuerys to retrieve context terms for each of them. For every ContextTermQuerys, context terms are returned by adding all other ContextTermQuerys to the query but itself. This means, if you want have various values for one index field, use one ContextTermQuery with a ValueList to submit the values instead of one ContextTermQuery for each value. If a QueryFilter.value == null, it is not used to restrict the query. Context terms for this ContextTermQuery are returned. The resulting list `ContextTermResults#contextTerms` is provided in the same order as the submitted list contextTermQueries

Extends `ESearchOptions`.

| Property | Type | Description |
|----------|------|-------------|
| `contextTermQueries` | ListOfContextTermQuery | List of contextTermQueries. |

## `ContextTermQuery`

Submit a ContextTermQuery for each ContextTerm to be retrieved. Describe the contextTerm in with filter. If a value is committed, this value is used to calculate every other ContextTerm in ContextTermOptions.contextTermQueries Specify a return type for each contextTerm: `ContextTermReturnTypeE` To exclude a value from contextTerms, use set isNegated=true. Defaults to false.

| Property | Type | Description |
|----------|------|-------------|
| `filter` | QueryFilter | Specify the field and type for which a context term should be returned. |
| `isNegated` | boolean | To exclude a value from contextTerms, use set isNegated=true. Defaults to false. |
| `returnType` | ContextTermReturnTypeE | Specify the return type of this ContextTerm. |

## `ContextTermResults`

| Property | Type | Description |
|----------|------|-------------|
| `contextTerms` | ListOfContextTerm |  |

## `ContextTermReturnTypeE`

Specifies the return type for context terms.

Values: `tokenized`, `notTokenized`, `numericSingleValues`, `numericBuckets`, `dateYearBuckets`

Controls the options of de.elo.ix.IXServicePortIF.copySord(). Copyright: Copyright (c) 2004 Organisation: ELO Digital Office GmbH

| Property | Type | Description |

| `adjustAclIgnoreDocuments` | boolean | Recursivly adjust the permissions of the child-documents of the moved object too. |
| `adjustAclIgnoreFolders` | boolean | Recursivly adjust the permissions of the sub-folders of the moved object too. |
| `adjustAclInBackground` | boolean | Recursivly adjust the permissions of the child-elements in the background. |
| `adjustAclOverwrite` | boolean | Set the permissions of the moved object equal to the permissions of the new parent. |
| `checkRecursion` | boolean | Check for recursion. If this member is set, the function `IXServicePortIF#copySord(ClientInfo, String, String, CopyInfo, CopySordZ)` checks whether the copy or move operation causes a recursion in … |
| `depth` | int32 | Depth up to which the structure is to be copied. THIS FUNCTION IS NOT CURRENTLY SUPPORTED! |
| `manSortIdx` | int32 | Manually determine the position. Use 0 to insert at the beginning of the child list. Use -1 to insert at the end of the child list ( this is the fastest mode). A value n greater then 0 inserts the … |
| `name` | string | COPY-Mode: Name of the destination archive entry. THIS FUNCTION IS NOT CURRENTLY SUPPORTED! |

## `CopyOptions`

Structure for the options for the copy-process. Copyright: Copyright (c) 2009 Organisation: ELO Digital Office GmbH

| Property | Type | Description |
|----------|------|-------------|
| `acceptChanges` | boolean | Accept the change. Only one of the three options keepOriginalPermissions, takeTargetPermissions and acceptChanges can be true. |
| `adjustAclDifference` | boolean | The permissions of the moved objects are modified. The permissions inherited from the old parent are substracted and the permissions of the new parent are added to the object. |
| `copyOnlyBaseElement` | boolean | Copy only the base element. |
| `copyOnlyWorkAttachment` | boolean | If inclAttachments and copyOnlyWorkAttachment are true, copy only the work attachments. Setting copyOnlyWorkAttachment to true when inclAttachments is false is invalid. |
| `copyOnlyWorkversion` | boolean | Do not copy all documents versions but only workversion |
| `copyStructuresAndDocuments` | boolean | Copy all structures with the documents. |
| `inclAttachments` | boolean | Also copy all attachments. |
| `keepCurrentNotes` | boolean | Keep the current documentnotes. |
| `keepOriginalOwner` | boolean | Keep the original owner of the archive element. |
| `keepOriginalPermissions` | boolean | Keep the current permissions. Only one of the three options keepOriginalPermissions, takeTargetPermissions and acceptChanges can be true. |
| `keepReferences` | boolean | Keep references to original objects in copied parents. This member is ignored, if ProcessInfo.inclReferences=false. If this member is true or only the parent is included in the tree, the copied … |
| `moveOnly` | boolean | Only move the element. |
| `newParentId` | int32 | ParentId of the new element. |
| `takeTargetPermissions` | boolean | Take the target permissions. Only one of the three options keepOriginalPermissions, takeTargetPermissions and acceptChanges can be true. |
| `targetName` | string | Name of the target archive element. |

## `DateHistogramAggregationQueryParams`

Defines parameters to run an aggregation of type "Date_Histogram" (a multi-bucket-aggregation). This aggregation query allows sub-aggregations.

Extends `AggregationQueryParams`.

| Property | Type | Description |
|----------|------|-------------|
| `bucketKeyDateFormat` | BucketKeyDateFormatE | Specifies the format of returned values of the key names of buckets. If this is not set, the default format is used "yyyMMddHHmmssSSS", which is the default ELO ISODate format. |
| `calendarInterval` | CalendarIntervalE | Specifies the width of generated buckets measured in time units (e.g. month). Multiple quantities, such as 6months, are not supported by ES. |
| `extendedBounds` | DateRangeValue | Sets optional extended bounds if parameter minDocCount equals to zero. Explanation: By default the histogram returns all the buckets within the range of the data itself, that is, the documents with … |
| `hardBounds` | DateRangeValue | The optional hard_bounds is a counterpart of extended_bounds and can limit the range of buckets in the histogram. It is particularly useful in the case of not filtered time ranges that can result in … |
| `minDocCount` | int32 | If minDocCount = 0, the response will fill gaps in the histogram with empty buckets. It is possible change that and request buckets with a higher minimum count by means of the min_doc_count setting. … |
| `missingValue` | DateIsoValue | The optional missingValue parameter defines how documents that are missing a value should be treated. By default they will be ignored but it is also possible to treat them as if they have a value. … |
| `offset` | DateRelative | Provides an optional offset to change the start value of each bucket by the specified positive (+) or negative offset (-) duration. For example, when using an interval of day, each bucket runs from … |
| `order` | AggregationOrderE | The optional order buckets are retrieved. By default, the date_histogram aggregation orders buckets by their key ascending. If this value is omitted, the default will be used. |
| `subAggregations` | MapToAggregationQuery | Inner aggregations of this aggregation. |

## `DateIsoValue`

Class to commit a date value to iSearch via QueryFilter. Date format: YYYYMMddHHmmss If less than 14 digits are commited, it is converted to a 14 digit numer, e.g.: "2017" -> "20170101000000" If roundTo is null, a search for an exact date is executed. If roundTo is != null, DateIsoValue is converted to a Range query. From is dateIso rounded down, To is dateIso rounded up. Example `dateIso=2017, roundTo=DateRoundC.YEAR` results in From=01.01.2017 00:00:00, To=31.12.2017 23:59:59 The delivered date is converted to the time zone submitted in ClientInfo

Extends `DateSingleValue`.

| Property | Type | Description |
|----------|------|-------------|
| `dateIso` | string | ISO-Date. Number of digits defines kind of timeunit: 4 digits for year. 6 digits for year with month. 8 digits for date with days. 10 digits for date with hours. 12 digits for date with minutes. 14 … |
| `roundTo` | DateRoundE | Define to which time unit this date should be rounded. If it is != null, a range query is performed. |

## `DateListValue`

A list of date values which is joined by AND or OR depending on `andOpeDoubleListrator`. Defaults to an OR-disjunction.

Extends `FilterValue`.

| Property | Type | Description |
|----------|------|-------------|
| `andOperator` | boolean | Connective of list elements (default is OR (=false) ). |
| `valueList` | ListOfDateValue | A list of double values which is joined by AND or OR depending on `andOperator`. |

## `DateNowValue`

Class to commit a time which refers to the current time. Depending on the committed values, different searches are performed: If `roundTo == null` and `relative == null`, a search for the exact current times is performed. If `roundTo != null` and `relative == null`, a range query is performed. From is current time rounded down, To is current time rounded up, defined by `roundTo`. If `relative != null`: A range query relative to current time is performed. If the biggest relative value is negative, a range query into the past is performed. `From` is current time - `relative`, rounded down if `roundTo != null`. `To` is current time, rounded up if `roundTo != null`. If the biggest relative value is positive, a range query into the future is performed. `From` is current time, rounded down if `roundTo != null`. `To` is current time + `relative`, rounded to if `roundTo != null`. The delivered date is converted to the time zone submitted in ClientInfo A time offset to UTC time can be delivered by `diffToUtc`. `SearchFieldE#X_DATE`, `SearchFieldE#I_DATE`, `SearchFieldE#DELETED_DATE` and `SearchFieldE#TIMESTAMP` are converted to UTC prior to saving them, index lines in date format are not converted. Therefore, a timezone should always be commited for these four.

Extends `DateSingleValue`.

| Property | Type | Description |
|----------|------|-------------|
| `relative` | DateRelative | Relative time differences to now. Can be null. |
| `roundTo` | DateRoundE | Define to which time unit this date should be rounded. If it is != null, a range query is performed. |

## `DateRangeValue`

Perform a range query with date values. Range queries for dates: If from contains a value for roundTo, it is always rounded down by roundTo. If to contains a value for roundTo, it is always rounded up by roundTo. If one of from or to is of type DateNowValue and contains a value for relative, it is always ignored for range queries. To perform an open range query (e.g. from now to infinite), only submit either a value for from or to

Extends `DateValue`.

| Property | Type | Description |
|----------|------|-------------|
| `from` | DateSingleValue |  |
| `to` | DateSingleValue |  |

## `DateRelative`

Can be used in three cases with different meanings: (1) Provides relative time differences for DateNowValues in search queries. If a range query into the past or future is performed, is defined if the biggest delivered value is positive or negative. e.g.: If `year=0, month=-2`, a range query from now to two months into the past is performed. e.g.: If `year=0, month=-2, days=10`, a range query from now to one month, 20 days into the past is performed. (2) Provides optional time offset for date histogram aggregations. Here, the time unit must be smaller than the requested calendar interval unit and it is only one field allowed which not equals to zero. So, you cannot add values with different units like in search queries. e.g.: If the requested interval is "day", the offset unit can only be smaller than day, e.g. "hour". (3) Provides date math for optional "extended boundary" and "hard boundary" parameters for date histogram aggregations. Here, it is only one field allowed which not equals to zero. So, you cannot add values with different units like in search queries.

| Property | Type | Description |
|----------|------|-------------|
| `day` | int32 | Time shift for days. example: -3 means three days to the past. 3 means three days to the future. |
| `hour` | int32 | Time shift for hours. example: -3 means three hours to the past. 3 means three hours to the future. |
| `minute` | int32 | Time shift for minutes. example: -3 means three minutes to the past. 3 means three minutes to the future. |
| `month` | int32 | Time shift for months. example: -3 means three months to the past. 3 means three months to the future. |
| `year` | int32 | Time shift for years. example: -3 means three years to the past. 3 means three years to the future. |

## `DateRoundE`

Enum for rounding DateValues. It defines if a date should be rounded to year, month, day, hour or minute.

Values: `NONE`, `YEAR`, `MONTH`, `DAY`, `HOUR`, `MINUTE`

| `documentMask` | boolean | Mask can be used to edit indexing information of a document. |

| `folderMask` | boolean | Mask can be used to edit indexing information of a folder |

| `inheritAclDisabled` | boolean | Do not inherit permissions from parent Sords. |
| `keywordingRelationAllowed` | boolean | Mask can be referenced from a DocMaskLine of type `DocMaskLineC#TYPE_RELATION`. If this mask has a data organisation `DocMaskC#DATA_ORGANISATION_ASPECT` the value determines if it can be referenced … |
| `region` | boolean | Sords of this mask, in case they are a folder, establish a region within the repository tree. This flag is only valid for masks of data organisation. `DocMaskC#DATA_ORGANISATION_ASPECT`. The region … |
| `releaseDocument` | boolean | Documents using this mask may be used as release notes |
| `searchMask` | boolean | Mask can be used to search for indexing information. |

| `translateSordName` | boolean | If `true` translate the sord's short description into or from the user language. |

## `DocMaskLine`

This class contains data for a line in the document mask.

| Property | Type | Description |
|----------|------|-------------|

| `acl` | string | Access control for mask line. |
| `aclItems` | AclItem[] |  |
| `allowedMaskIdsForKeywordingRelation` | ListOfInteger | Specifies the `DocMask#id` allowed in a `DocMaskLineC#TYPE_RELATION`. Mandatory if DocMaskLine is of type `DocMaskLineC#TYPE_RELATION`. Only Sords with the specified `DocMask#id` can be referenced in … |
| `canEdit` | boolean | Determines whether the index line can be modified. Read-only. |
| `comment` | string | Quickinfo text for the attribute. |
| `commentTranslationKey` | string | Translation-keyword for `DocMaskLine#comment`. |
| `defaultValue` | string | This value is assigned to the ObjKey.data element for a new Sord object. |
| `disableWordWheel` | boolean | Disable word wheel. Clients should not provide a function to list all existing values for this index vlaue. The Indexserver does not set or test this value. |
| `editCol` | int32 | Column position of edit control |
| `editRow` | int32 | Row postion of edit control |
| `editWidth` | int32 | With of edit control |
| `excludeFromISearch` | boolean | If true a index value will not be searchable via iSearch. Does not affect the index search. |
| `externalData` | string | External data. Can be used to store an arbitary string. |
| `hidden` | boolean | This value should not be displayed to the user. |
| `id` | int32 | The ID of the line information. |
| `important` | boolean | Display index value next to the objects short description. This element has to be interpreted by the client application. Indexserver ignores this value. |
| `inherit` | boolean | Inherit this keywording field to subordinated entries. This option is used to make sure, that all entries in an archive sub-tree have the same value for this keywording field. Thereby the field is … |
| `inheritFromParent` | boolean | Do not inherit the parent's value. |
| `key` | string | The attribute group name. This member corresponds to `ObjKey.name`. |
| `labelCol` | int32 | Column position of label control |
| `labelRow` | int32 | Row position of label control |
| `maskId` | int32 | This line information belongs to a storage mask with the ID MaskId. |
| `max` | int32 | The maximum value of this attribute. |
| `min` | int32 | The minimum value of this attribute. |
| `name` | string | The name of the attribute. This value is displayed in the lable before the edit field. It can be translated into reps. from the users language: set `SessionOptionsC.TRANSLATE_TERM`. |
| `nameTranslationKey` | string | Translation-keyword for `DocMaskLine#name`. |
| `nextTab` | boolean | Creates a new tab in the keywording dialogue. |
| `notTokenized` | boolean | Defines whether a index value should be searchable as TOKENIZED or UN_TOKENIZED |
| `onlyBuzzwords` | boolean | The index line can only contain buzzwords. |
| `postfixAsterix` | boolean | Add "*" after index value in search operation. |
| `prefixAsterix` | boolean | Add "*" before index value in search operation. |
| `readOnly` | boolean | This value should not be edited in a user interface (convention). Scripts are allowed to edit the value. |
| `required` | boolean | Entry is required. |
| `serverScriptName` | string | A script or plugin at the server can serve as the data source of a dynamic keyword list. This value represents the name of that script or plugin. If the value is not empty, the client can request a … |
| `tabIndex` | int32 | This value represents the number of the tab, this line has to appear on. The first tab's index value is 0. It is on the client's hand to set valid values when checking in document masks. |
| `tabOrder` | int32 | Tabulator order of edit control |
| `translate` | boolean | Translate index values into or from the users language. |
| `type` | int32 | The type of the line information. This can be one of the `DocMaskLineC. TYPE_*` constants. |
| `validateExpression` | string | RegEx to validate user input for entry. |
| `valueArray` | boolean | Allows multiple values for keywording tables. For keywording forms organized as tables, see `DocMaskC#DATA_ORGANISATION_TABLE`, index lines must be explicitly declared for storing multiple values. If … |
| `version` | boolean | Display index value in version information dialog when checking in a document. This element has to be interpreted by the client application. Indexserver ignores this value. |

| `nameTranslationKey` | string | Translation-keyword for `DocMaskLine#name`. |
| `notTokenized` | boolean | Defines whether a index value should be searchable as TOKENIZED or UN_TOKENIZED |
| `onlyBuzzwords` | boolean | The index line can only contain buzzwords. |
| `postfixAsterix` | boolean | Add "*" after index value in search operation. |
| `prefixAsterix` | boolean | Add "*" before index value in search operation. |
| `readOnly` | boolean | This value should not be edited in a user interface (convention). Scripts are allowed to edit the value. |
| `required` | boolean | Entry is required. |
| `serverScriptName` | string | A script at the server can serve as the data source of a dynamic keyword list. This value represents the name of that script. If the value is not empty, the client can request a list of available … |
| `translate` | boolean | Translate index values into or from the users language. |
| `type` | int32 | The type of the line information. This can be one of the `DocMaskLineC. TYPE_*` constants. |
| `validateExpression` | string | RegEx to validate user input for entry. |
| `version` | boolean | Display index value in version information dialog when checking in a document. This element has to be interpreted by the client application. Indexserver ignores this value. |

| `URL_PARAM_LAST_ACCESS` | string | URL parameter last access date. Used to set the last access time of the file. ISO date in UTC timezone. Example: String writeUrl = docVersion.url + "&" + URL_PARAM_LAST_ACCESS + "=" + 20010203040506; |
| `URL_PARAM_LAST_UPDATE` | string | URL parameter last write date. Used to set the last write time of the file. ISO date in UTC timezone. Example: String writeUrl = docVersion.url + "&" + URL_PARAM_LAST_UPDATE + "=" + 20010203040506; |

| `URL_PARAM_OFFSET` | string | URL parameter offset. Used to read a file at a particular offset. Do not position offset after the length of the file. Example: String readUrl = docVersion.url + "&" + URL_PARAM_OFFSET + "=" + 123; |

| `lnExt` | int32 | Length of file extension. |
| `lnVersion` | int32 | Length of version number (like 1.0). |

Use this class to search for documents by `de.elo.ix.client.IXServicePortIF#findFirstSords(de.elo.ix.client.ClientInfo, de.elo.ix.client.FindInfo, int, de.elo.ix.client.SordZ)` and `de.elo.ix.client.IXServicePortIF#findNextSords(de.elo.ix.client.ClientInfo, String, int, int, de.elo.ix.client.SordZ)`. | Property | Type | Description |
|----------|------|-------------|

| `highlightedText` | boolean | Return highlighted result text for each sord. Highlighted text is retrieved from fulltext and surrounded by b-tags. Defaults to false. |
| `relevance` | boolean | Calculate relevance for each sord. Defaults to false. |
| `resultField` | boolean | Return fields in which search terms are found for each sord. Defaults to false. |
| `sort` | ESearchOrderE | Results are ordered by this parameter. |

This class defines how to connect to a domain controller, login a user and find her groups.

| Property | Type | Description |
|----------|------|-------------|
| `AD_ATTRIBUTE_MAIL` | string | LDAP attribute ID that specifies the mail address of a given user. |
| `AD_ATTRIBUTE_MANAGER` | string | AD attribute ID -manager- that specifies the superior of an ELO user. |
| `AD_ATTRIBUTE_MEMBER_OF` | string | LDAP attribute ID that specifies the group list of a given user or group. |
| `AD_SAM_ACCOUNT_NAME` | string | LDAP attribute ID for sAMAccountName. |
| `AD_USER_PRINCIPAL_NAME` | string | LDAP attribute ID for userPrincipalName. |
| `DEFAULT_ATTRIBUTES` | string[] |  |
| `DEFAULT_ENVIRONMENT` | Array2OfString | Default properties for LDAP connection via Java. |
| `FILTER_GROUPS` | string | Filter expression used to find groups of a given user. |
| `FILTER_PERSON` | string | Filter expression used to find a person by her/his sAMAccountName. |
| `FILTER_PERSON_MAIL` | string | Filter expression used to find a person by her mail address. |
| `FILTER_PERSON_USER_PRINCIPAL_NAME` | string | Filter expression used to find a person by her/his userPrinicpalName. |
| `attributeForUserPropOS` | string | LDAP attribute to be used as OS name for `UserInfoC#PROP_NAME_OS`. This value is either `#AD_SAM_ACCOUNT_NAME` or `#AD_USER_PRINCIPAL_NAME`. If set as `#AD_USER_PRINCIPAL_NAME`, also set … |
| `attributeNameMail` | string | Name of attribute that contains the user's mail address. Defaults to "mail". |
| `attributeNameMemberOf` | string | Name of attribute that contains the user's groups. Defaults to "memberOf". |
| `attributeSuperior` | string | Attribute to determine the superior of an ELO user. Set this attribute ID e.g. to `#AD_ATTRIBUTE_MANAGER` to assign the superior of a user on login. This value is empty by default, which means that … |
| `attributesToRead` | SetOfString | Read this LDAP attributes into ldapProperties of UserInfo. The attributes `#getAttributeNameMail()` and `#getAttributeNameMemberOf()` are always read and need not to be included in this collection. |
| `connectionTimeoutSeconds` | int32 | Timeout limit when connecting to a LDAP server. |
| `defaultUserName` | string | Optional user name to connect to the LDAP servers. This account must have the permission to list the group associations of all users. It does not need to be an ELO account too. |
| `defaultUserPasswordEncr` | string | Optional encrypted user password to connect to the LDAP servers. This password can also be set in plain text. It is automatically encrypted when stored into a file or database. |
| `domainPrefix` | string | Domain prefix. The login name of the user is prefixed by this value to build the user property `UserInfoC#PROP_NAME_OS`. When using this prefix, users should also use this prefix for login. This … |
| `groupSearchDNList` | ListOfString | Search paths to find groups. If empty, only those groups are assigned to the user in ELO the user is a direct member of in LDAP. If not empty, the user's groups are recursively searched through group … |
| `initialContextEnvironment` | MapToString | Additional properties to build the login environment. Example: initialContextEnvironment.put(Context.SECURITY_AUTHENTICATION, "DIGEST-MD5"); |
| `maxGroupNesting` | int32 | Groups are collected recursively up to this depth. A value of 0 means no depth limit. This value is ignored, if `#groupSearchDNList` is empty. |
| `name` | string | Domain name. E.g. ELO. LOCAL |
| `nameFormatter` | string | Format string to build a unique ELO account name from LDAP attributes. LDAP attributes have to be enclosed in $. Examples: 1. $cn$, $department$ ; 2. $sAMAccountName$ $department$ |
| `parentOfNewUser` | string | Assign this user as parent for a new ELO user. ID, GUID or name of existing ELO user or group that is assigned as UserInfo#parent when a new ELO user is created. This value is empty by default, which … |
| `personSearchDNList` | ListOfString | Search paths to find persons. Only users that are found in one of this DN can login to ELO. E.g. "cn=users,dc=elotest2,dc=local". |
| `requiredGroup` | string | Users must be member of this LDAP group. Optional, short LDAP group name (not a DN) the user must be a member of in order to login. The membership in this group is checked after the group list has … |
| `searchFilterForGroups` | string | Name of attribute that contains the user's groups. Defaults to "(&(objectCategory=group)(member={0}))" |
| `searchFilterForMail` | string | Search filter for mail address. Defaults to "(&(objectclass=person)(mail={0}))" |
| `searchFilterForPerson` | string | Search filter for person. Defaults to "(&(objectclass=person)(sAMAccountName={0}))" |
| `searchTimeLimitSeconds` | int32 | Search for groups can least up to this number of seconds. |
| `servers` | ListOfServer | List of LDAP servers of the domain. |

## `DoubleListValue`

A list of double values which is joined by AND or OR depending on `andOperator`. Defaults to an OR-disjunction.

Extends `FilterValue`.

| Property | Type | Description |
|----------|------|-------------|
| `andOperator` | boolean | Connective of list elements (default is OR (=false) ). |
| `valueList` | ListOfDoubleValue | A list of double values which is joined by AND or OR depending on `andOperator`. |

## `DoubleRangeValue`

Class to commit a range filter of double values or specify a range aggregation. To perform an open range query (e.g. from now to infinite), only submit either a value for from or to To perform a range aggregation, submit both from/to or at least from or to for each requested bucket (e.g. [from 3.14 to 6.28] or [from 3.14 to infinite]). Note that this aggregation includes the "from"-value and excludes the "to"-value for each range.

Extends `DoubleValue`.

| Property | Type | Description |
|----------|------|-------------|
| `from` | DoubleSingleValue |  |
| `to` | DoubleSingleValue |  |

## `DoubleSingleValue`

Extends `DoubleValue`.

| Property | Type | Description |
|----------|------|-------------|
| `value` | double | double value |

## `DoubleValue`

Class to commit a numeric double value to iSearch via QueryFilter.

Extends `FilterValue`.

| `completion` | EsSettingsProperty | Query "auto completion" on search |

| `continueReindexOnFailure` | EsSettingsProperty | Define if reindex process should be continued or interrupted on failure. |
| `correction` | EsSettingsProperty | Query "corrections" on search |
| `feedAnalyzer` | EsSettingsProperty | Define if feed should use archive language, one of the configured languages or try to determine the language based on content. |
| `germanAnalyzer` | EsSettingsProperty | Option to define a analyzer for German language without decomposition (to speed up analysis process, but results in worse search results). Default: empty or "elo_german". Set to … |
| `hosts` | EsSettingsProperty | Elasticsearch host address. |
| `indexName` | EsSettingsProperty | Elasticsearch index name |
| `indexingDisabled` | EsSettingsProperty | Define if this IX instance should be used to run reindex and updater thread. Can only be set instance specific. |
| `indexingOfMetadataFieldsViaKeywordingRelationEnabled` | EsSettingsProperty | EIX-1567: Erlaubt das Indizieren von markierten Indexfeldern von Sords, die über Felder vom Typ RELATION referenziert werden. Die markierten Felder der so referenzierten Sords werden mit im … |
| `instanceName` | string |  |
| `maxAmountOfReferencesInErrorFolder` | EsSettingsProperty | Maximum number of references created in Reindex error folder during reindex process |
| `maxFulltextLength` | EsSettingsProperty | Define the maximun length for fulltext documents indexed by iSearch. If not set or set to <= 0, complete documents are indexed. |
| `maxObjsPerReindexInterval` | EsSettingsProperty | Maximum number of objects to be processed in one time unit of updater. Default: 1.000. Set to -1 if nothing should be changed. |
| `maxObjsPerUpdateInterval` | EsSettingsProperty | Maximum number of objects selected by reindexer. Default: 10. 000 Set to -1 if nothing should be changed. |
| `maxSearchResults` | EsSettingsProperty | Define how many results should be returned with findFirstSords and findNextSords (in total!) by iSearch. Defaults to 10000. |
| `maxTokenLength` | EsSettingsProperty | Maximum length of tokens in elastic search. Defaults to 300, max 10922. Set to -1 if nothing should be changed. |
| `nbOfAllowedLangs` | int32 | Number of allowed languages. Immutable. |
| `nodeName` | EsSettingsProperty | Elasticsearch node name |
| `numberOfShards` | EsSettingsProperty | Number of shards per index in Elasticsearch. Default: 1. |
| `numberOfShardsByMaskId` | EsSettingsProperty | Define the nuber of shards for individual masks. Use a higher number of (e.g. 3) for masks with many objects. Masks which are not defined with this setting use numberOfShards. Syntax: maskId:number … |
| `reindexFromObjId` | EsSettingsProperty | Reindex processes object IDs >= this one. Set to -1 to delete, everything >= 0 is written to db (if lower than reindexToObjId) Always needs to send reindexFromObjId and reindexToObjId. |
| `reindexStarted` | string | Reindex started at this time. Format: yyyyMMddHHmmss Timezone: JVM default. |
| `reindexToObjId` | EsSettingsProperty | Reindex processes object IDs <= this one. Set to -1 to delete, everything >= 0 is written to db (if greater than reindexFromObjId) Always needs to send reindexFromObjId and reindexToObjId. |
| `searchAnalyzer` | EsSettingsProperty | Define a special analyzer to use during search. Please make sure the defined analyzer exists. Remove or set to "default" to disable. |
| `skipNonAsciiStepsOnFallback` | EsSettingsProperty | Determines if we should skip some analyzer steps when we use the fallback analyzer. The skipped steps are trimming non-ascii chars from the analyzed text. Setting this option to true results in using … |
| `synonyms` | EsSettingsProperty | Query synonyms on search |
| `updaterFromTs` | EsSettingsProperty | Process documents newer than this date. Format: yyyyMMddHHmmss Timezone: JVM default. |
| `updaterInterval` | EsSettingsProperty | How often should the updater run? Interval in seconds |
| `updaterNextStart` | EsSettingsProperty | Next start of updater. Format: yyyyMMddHHmmss. Timezone: JVM default. |
| `useSsl` | boolean | Use SSL encryption. |

## `ESSettingsObj`

checkoutEsSettings returns the current IX instance name, a list of all available IX instances as well as one EsInstanceSettings object for every IX instance of the archive and one for the setting "_ALL". If there is no entry for a setting in the database, the default value is returned as value. checkinEsSettings writes entries for every EsInstanceSettings to the database: If a EsSettingsProperty member is null, nothing is written or deleted. To delete a setting, the EsSettingsProperty member must be set, but its member 'value' must be set to 'null'

| Property | Type | Description |
|----------|------|-------------|
| `allInstanceNames` | ArrayListOfString | List of all instance names |
| `instanceName` | string | Name of this IX instance |
| `instanceSettings` | ArrayListOfESInstanceSettings | List of instance settings. Contains settings (as in database) for instance _ALL and every IX instance. |

## `ESStatusObj`

Calling checkoutEsStatus returns this object to inform about the current status of connection to Elasticsearch and running processes.

| Property | Type | Description |
|----------|------|-------------|
| `esConnection` | boolean | Is this IX connected to the Elasticsearch server. |
| `lastExceptionReindexer` | string | Last exception of reindexer of this IX |
| `lastExceptionUpdater` | string | Last exception of updater of this IX |
| `runningReindexers` | MapToString | List of all IXs with an active reindex process. It returns the IX ID and its progress percent. |
| `runningUpdaters` | MapToString | List of all IXs with an active updater process. It returns the IX ID and its progress percent. |

## `ESearchParams`

Parameters for iSearch.

| Property | Type | Description |
|----------|------|-------------|
| `inclDeleted` | int32 | Define if deleted sords should be included: 0: exclude deleted sords (default). 1: include deleted sords. 2: only deleted sords. |
| `query` | string | Query String. Note: Do not escaped any characters, this is done by IX. A Power Search is enabled if query starts with = . In this case, nothing is escaped by IX. |
| `queryOperator` | QueryOperator | The QueryOperator is a combination of various QueryFilters to specify in which fields (e.g. index field, sord title, ...) |

## `Event`

This class represents an event used by the event bus API functions.

| Property | Type | Description |
|----------|------|-------------|
| `acl` | string | Access control list. An empty ACL means here, that the event is dispatched to everyone. |
| `any` | Any | Event payload data as Any object. |
| `busId` | string | Bus-ID |
| `data` | string | Event payload data as byte array. |
| `id` | string | Event ID. If the sender awaits a response to this event, it should set a random number in `id`. The receiver should use the same number in the response. |
| `param1` | string | Value depends on event type. Event filters check this value. |
| `param2` | string | Value depends on event type. Event filters check this value. |
| `senderId` | string | User ID of event sender. Might be used for other IDs in future too. Read-only. |
| `type` | string | Event type. Event filters check this value. This must be one of the predefined event types or an application defined type with a larger value than EventBusC.EVENT_TYPE_MAX_SYSTEM. |

## `EventBusC`

Constants related to the event bus API.

| Property | Type | Description |
|----------|------|-------------|
| `BUSID_BROADCAST` | string | Broadcast bus ID. The EventBusC. BUSID_BROADCAST refers to a public communication channel that can used by all applications and users. Any user can send events to this bus and is allowed to listen to … |
| `BUSID_MAX_SYSTEM` | string | Reserved. |
| `BUSID_USER` | string | User related bus ID. For each user, an event bus exists with the bus ID EventBusC. BUSID_USER + user-ID. Any user can send events to this bus but only the owning user can listen to it. |
| `EVENT_FULLTEXT_INDEXING_STATUS_UPDATE` | string | Notify about status change in fulltext indexing. |
| `EVENT_OCR_REQUEST` | string | OCR Request Event. OCR Worker Processes listen to this event and process OCR on the supplied image data. In order to listen for this event, the session user must have the permission FLAG_ADMIN. |
| `EVENT_OCR_RESULT` | string | OCR Finished Event. OCR Worker Process sends this event if the OCR processing is finished. The client application initiates a OCR request by calling API function processOCR. It receives an Event ID |
| `EVENT_TYPE_CHAT` | string | Chat event type. Events of this type contain chat text. |
| `EVENT_TYPE_CLOSE_CHAT` | string | Close chat event type. An event of this type is sent to notify the subscribers that the chat channel (resp. event bus) will be closed shortly. |
| `EVENT_TYPE_MAX_SYSTEM` | string | User defined events must have a higher type value than this limit. |
| `EVENT_TYPE_OPEN_CHAT` | string | Open chat event type. Events of this type are used to invite subscribers to open a public final static chat channel. |
| `EVENT_TYPE_WATCH_FOLDER` | string | Watch folder event. An event of this type is sent, if the contents of a given folder have been changed. In order to register a listener for such events, the folder to be watched has to be specified … |
| `WATCH_DELETE` | int32 | An object was deleted. |
| `WATCH_INSERT` | int32 | An object was inserted. |
| `WATCH_UPDATE` | int32 | An object was updated, locked or unlocked. |

## `EventBusParams`

This class supplies params to control the creation of an event bus ID. Only one of the members ticket, userId and channelId should be set.

| Property | Type | Description |
|----------|------|-------------|
| `channelId` | string | Create an event bus ID based on this string. An arbitary string can be supplied. |
| `noForwardToOtherIxs` | boolean | Do not open this bus on other Indexservers. In load balancing environments, event busses are opened on each Indexserver by default. Set this option to true to open this event bus only on the … |
| `ticket` | string | Create event bus ID based on this ticket. |
| `userId` | string | Get the event bus ID for this user. A numeric ID, GUID or user name can be specified. The returned bus ID is computed as EventBusC.BUSID_USER + uid, where uid is the numeric user ID. |

## `Feed`

This class represents a document feed. Each Sord can have at most one Feed object. A Feed contains of a list of Action objects which can be user comments, comments generated by scripts and actions generated by the system. The Feed object acts like a header for the list of actions. Its main purpose is the mapping between feed actions and a Sord object. In order to receive a feed of a Sord object inclusive all actions, call function `FeedService#findFirstActions(de.elo.ix.client.ClientInfo, FindActionsInfo, int, ActionZ)`.

| Property | Type | Description |
|----------|------|-------------|
| `TStampSync` | string | Timestamp of this object's last export by the replication. |
| `createDateIso` | string | Create date. It holds the ISO formatted create date in the time zone of the client application. In order to convert this value into a date object, invoke function … |
| `guid` | string | Feed GUID. |
| `objGuid` | string | GUID of the associated Sord object. |
| `tStamp` | string | Time stamp. Time stamp of creation or modification. |

## `FeedPost`

A FeedPost contains a parent action (an action where action. parentGuid is empty) and all subactions (actions where action.parentGuid = parent action's guid). From all actions their information (e.g. hashtags, mentions) are accumulated into lists.

| Property | Type | Description |
|----------|------|-------------|
| `creatorIds` | HashSetOfInteger | List of all user IDs which created any action |
| `feedGuid` | string | GUID of corresponding feed (read from parentAction. feedGuid) |
| `hashTags` | HashSetOfString | List of all hashtags of actions if action.type == EActionType. UserComment |
| `lastChanged` | string | Date of last change of any action |
| `linkedGuids` | HashSetOfString | List of GUIDs which are linked in any action if action.type == EActionType. UserComment |
| `mentions` | HashSetOfString | List of all mentions (UserNames) of actions if action.type == EActionType. UserComment |
| `parentAction` | Action | an action where action. parentGuid is empty |
| `sordGuid` | string | GUID of corresponsing Sord |
| `subActions` | ListOfAction | List of all subActions (action.parentGuid = parentAction. guid) |
| `text` | string | Text of all actions, separated by a whitespace |
| `textList` | ListOfString | List of all texts of all actions |
| `versionComment` | string | Version comment of corresponding DocVersion if parentAction.getDocVersionGuid() is set. |
| `versionName` | string | Name of corresponding DocVersion if parentAction.getDocVersionGuid() is set. |

## `FindActionsInfo`

Describes search criteria for `FeedService#findFirstActions(de.elo.ix.client.ClientInfo, FindActionsInfo, int, ActionZ)`. If only objId is set, userId and createDateIso is empty, the entire document feed of the given object is returned. The (main) actions are sorted descending by create date. The answers (child actions) follow immediately their associated main action. Answers are sorted ascending by create date. If any other member is also set, or if objId is combined with another member, the search combines the elements by logical AND. The result list contains all actions sorted descending by create date. The ordering does not distinguish between main actions and child actions.

| Property | Type | Description |
|----------|------|-------------|
| `actionTypes` | ListOfEActionType | Search by action types. If this element is empty, actions of all types are returned. |
| `createDateIso` | string | Search by create date. A date range can be specified by using the default range delimiter "... " or the one defined in `de.elo.ix.client.FindOptions#getRangeDelimiter()`. The time part is assumed to … |

Constants for FindActionInfo and findFirstActions.

## `FindActivityInfo`

This class specifies constraints for the API function `IXServicePortIF#findFirstActivities(ClientInfo, FindActivityInfo, int, ActivityZ)`. By default, the members of this class are combined by AND. If `#senderOrReceiver` is set, senderId and receiverId are combined by OR. If neither senderId nor receiverId is set, the function findFirstActivities collects all activities that can be read by the current session.

| Property | Type | Description |
|----------|------|-------------|
| `inclDeleted` | boolean | Find activities for deleted folders and documents too. |
| `inclDeputy` | boolean | Search for the activities of the deputized users too. |
| `inclGroup` | boolean | Search for all groups of sender and receiver too. |
| `objId` | string | Find by object ID. Find activities assigned to this object. Optional. |
| `receiverId` | string | Find activities created for this receiver. Can be set to a user ID, user GUID or user name. Optional. |
| `senderId` | string | Find activities created by this user. Can be set to a user ID, user GUID or user name. Optional. |
| `senderOrReceiver` | boolean | Search for sender or receiver. Combine the senderId and receiverId by logical OR operation. |

## `FindByFulltext`

Performs a fulltext search. This class is deprecated. The fulltext search is performed via iSearch. Therefore, use `de.elo.ix.client.esearch.FindByESearch` instead. The `#term` should be replaced by the actual query term and additional filters (e.g. DocMask) should be submitted by `de.elo.ix.client.esearch.ESearchParams#queryOperator`. The areas in which should be search should be set in `de.elo.ix.client.esearch.ESearchParams#searchIn`. Searches using this class are still executed but internally mapped to `de.elo.ix.client.esearch.FindByESearch` objects and some values might be ignored. Furthermore, features or data structures introduced by ELO versions later than version 11 are not supported by this kind of search definition.

| Property | Type | Description |
|----------|------|-------------|
| `term` | string | Search term to be located in the fulltext database. Can be a single word or a number of words. Wildcard * is allowed, it matches any number of characters. Term is interpreted based on the options … |

## `FindByFulltextCtrl`

This class is used to search for objects that have to be indexed by the fulltext database engine. The selected objects are ordered by their timestamp. If the search process is interrupted because of a timeout or because the FindOptions.totalCount limit is reached, all the Objects of the lates timestamp second are discarded. If the search process is terminated due to breakTotalCount and the array of results is empty, the search should be repeated with a greater value for totalCount. Access checking is performed as in all other searches. A search of this type can only be combined with FindOptions.totalCount and FindOptions.timeoutSeconds. The results are returned in the member FindInfo.fulltextCtrlResultItems. The Sord objects are available in the member FindInfo.sords too.

| Property | Type | Description |
|----------|------|-------------|
| `endTStamp` | string | Find objects from this date or older. If the value is null or empty, the time range has no upper limit. |
| `onlyDocs` | boolean | Find next documents for textreader. Note: this field is currently unused |
| `profileKeyPrefix` | string | Key prefix for loop state. |
| `reindex` | boolean | Re-index processing. If this member is true, a search returns objects from the largest object ID to 2. If this member is false, a search returns the next objects in a timestamp interval. |
| `startTStamp` | string | Find objects from this date or newer. This value can be null or empty in which case the time range has no lower limit. |

## `FindByIndex`

Finds an object according to the object's index properties. The search terms are concatinated by the operator specified with FindOptions.searchMode. If FindOptionsC.OPERATOR_OR is the specified searchMode the members of this class are concatinated in the search string with the boolean operator "OR". Any other searchMode concatinates with "AND". Exception: userId and maskId are always used as "AND" terms. Copyright: Copyright (c) 2004 Organisation: ELO Digital Office GmbH

| `confidentiality` | string | Find objects with this confidentiality level. If the confidentiality is to be ignored this value must be set to `null`. To get the list of available confidentiality levels, use … |
| `delDateIso` | string | Find objects with this due date. It is a single date value or a date range in ISO format. |
| `deleteDateIso` | string | Find objects with this delete date. It is a single date value or a date range in ISO format. |
| `deleteUser` | string | Find objects deleted by the user with this user ID or name. If the deleted user is to be ignored this value must be set to null. |
| `desc` | string | Find objects with this term included in the description of the object (case insensitive). This value can be a list of terms separated by blanks. The terms are concatinated with the operator specified … |
| `exactName` | boolean | If this option is false, all objects are found that contain the term specified in `name` anywhere in their short description (Sord.name). In other words: a search by `*name*` is executed. Set this … |
| `externalId` | string | Find objects with this externalId. |
| `iDateIso` | string | Find objects with this internal date. It is a single date value or a date range in ISO format. |
| `includeChildMasks` | boolean | Set this flag to `true` if you want to include masks that inherit from the specified masks in `#maskId` and `#maskIds`. |
| `kind` | string | Colour. If the colour is to be ignored this value must be set to null. |
| `lockId` | string | Set this value to an user's id or name to search for objects currently locked by that user. To ignore this option, set this value to `null` (default). |

## `FindByType`

This class holds additional information for FindInfo, in order to restrict a search using document types. The default resolving sequence is ordered by the grade of restriction: 1. typeIDs, typeNames, typeExtensions is the most specialised information, 2. typeDocuments containing all document types (IDs, Names, Extensions), 3. and typeStructures including all levels of structure elements. 4. If none of the parameters above is valid, the complete restriction FindByType is omitted. Copyright: Copyright (c) 2004 Organisation: ELO Digital Office GmbH

| `typeStructures` | boolean | Include all structure elements |

## `FindTranslateTermInfo`

This class is used to find translations of terms. Localization information is stored in two kinds of storage: in the database and in property files in the repository. The former kind use the database table `translations` which has a column for each language. This localization data can be modified or extended via API function `IXServicePortIF#checkinTranslateTerms(ClientInfo, TranslateTerm[], LockZ)`. The latter kind stores localization as property files under /Administration/Localization. This data can only be changed via checkin/out and takes effect only after Indexserver re-start. Indexserver function `IXServicePortIF#findFirstTranslateTerms(ClientInfo, FindTranslateTermInfo, int)` searches over both localization sources and returns matches from both. The translations found in the database are returned first. EIX-2912: Since IX version 21.3, a language is specified as IETF language tag (lang). The language part is from ISO 639-1. It is optionally followed by a country tag from ISO 3166-1 alpha-2. The parts are separated by hyphen. Examples: de-DE, de-CH, de-AT. The function tries to find the translation for a given lang in the following order: 1. lang as defined, e.g. de-CH 2. lang without country, e.g. de 3. first lang with the same language tag, e.g. de-DE 4. first system language

| Property | Type | Description |
|----------|------|-------------|
| `disableFallback` | boolean | Disable fallback to default language if a key in `#translationKeyPrefixes` is not found. If there is no translation term associated to a key for a language in `#langs`, an empty String is returned in … |
| `inclDeleted` | boolean | Reserved - Find deleted terms too (currently not implemented). |
| `inclToBeTranslated` | boolean | Return those terms too, that should be translated into other languages: e.g. Keywording form names (DocMask.name), index value lables (DocMaskLine.name) and Keywords. |
| `includeAllLevels` | boolean | Find terms of lower or equal the level. |
| `langs` | string[] |  |
| `level` | int32 | Find terms of this priority level. If 0 (default), return the terms with the highest level. |
| `packageName` | string | Package GUID. |
| `terms` | string[] |  |
| `translationKeyPrefixes` | string[] |  |

This class describes the search criteria for `IXServicePortIF#findFirstUsers`. The wildcards defined by `SessionOptionsC#DB_WILDCARDS` can be used for `#name`, `#desc`, `#property`, and `#ldapProperty`. Members `#name`, `#desc`, `#property`, and `#ldapProperty` are combined by OR. Other members are combinded by AND.

| `findResultAccessMode` | FindResultAccessMode | Describes how the client application will access the results. |

| `hasFlags2` | int32 | Select only users that have all of this `UserInfo#flags2` set. A value of 0 is ignored. The selected users are found by testing (UserInfo#flags2 & hasFlags2) == hasFlags2. |
| `hasNotFlags` | int32 | Select only users that have non this `UserInfo#flags` set. The selected users are found by testing (UserInfo#flags & hasNotFlags) == 0. |
| `hasNotFlags2` | int32 | Select only users that have none this `UserInfo#flags2` set. The selected users are found by testing (UserInfo#flags2 & hasNotFlags2) == 0. |

| `name` | string | Find by user name. Search over `UserInfo#name`. |
| `onlyGroups` | boolean | Search for groups only. If this member is set to `true`, flags provided by `#checkoutUsersZ` are ignored. |
| `onlyUsers` | boolean | Search for users only. If this member is set to `true`, flags provided by `#checkoutUsersZ` are ignored. |
| `orgUnit` | string | Selects all members of a given OrgUnit. The `OrgUnitInfo#id` is expected as input. Equivalent to `CheckoutUsersC#MEMBERS_OF_ORGUNIT` |
| `packageName` | string[] |  |
| `property` | KeyValue | Find by an user property. Search over all `UserInfo#userProps`. The key element in property is the index in the array `UserInfo#userProps`. Example: FindUserInfo findInfo = new FindUserInfo(); … |

| `sortOrder` | int32 | Specify how the results should be ordered. Valid values are `SortOrderC#USERNAME` and `SortOrderC#IUSERNAME`. Sorted results are returned in FindResult#sortedResult. |
| `subadmin` | string[] |  |

## `FixedPointNumberRangeValue`

Class to commit a range filter of fixed-point number values or specify a range aggregation. To perform an open range query (e.g. from now to infinite), only submit either a value for from or to To perform a range aggregation, submit both from/to or at least from or to for each requested bucket (e.g. [from 3.14 to 6.28] or [from 3.14 to infinite]). Note that this aggregation includes the "from"-value and excludes the "to"-value for each range.

Extends `FixedPointNumberValue`.

| Property | Type | Description |
|----------|------|-------------|
| `from` | FixedPointNumberSingleValue |  |
| `to` | FixedPointNumberSingleValue |  |
