## `ForwardSubstitutionInfo`

Forward a substitution from the its current substitute to a new substitute. A substitution can either be forwarded or transfered. Forward a substitution : The original substitution is not altered. An additional `Substitution` object is created by copying the original one and setting the new substitute. If `SubstitutionSettings#canBeActivatedManually` is set to `true`, an arbitrary number of SubstitutionPeriods (also 0) can be provided. The original ones are replaced with the new ones. If `SubstitutionSettings#canBeActivatedManually` is set to `false`, all provided SubstitutionPeriods must be sub-periods of the original ones. Set `#transferSubstitution` to false to forward a substitution. Transfer a substitution : A new `Substitution` object is created by copying it from the original one, only replacing the `Substitution#substituteId` by the provided new substitute. The original substitution is deleted. Set `#transferSubstitution` to true to transfer a substitution.

| Property | Type | Description |
|----------|------|-------------|
| `newSubstituteId` | int32 | Id of the new substitute. This value is ignored if `#newSubstituteName` is set. |
| `newSubstituteName` | string | User name of the new substitute. `#newSubstituteId` is ignored if this member is set. |
| `substitutionGuid` | string | GUID of substitution to be forwarded or transfered. |
| `substitutionPeriods` | ListOfSubstitutionPeriod | New substitution periods is a substitution if forwarded. Must be set if a substitution is forwarded (set `#transferSubstitution` to false) and is ignored if a substitution if transfered (set … |
| `transferSubstitution` | boolean | Define if the substitution should be forwarded or transfered. |

## `HistogramAggregationQueryParams`

Defines parameters to run an aggregation of type "Histogram" on numeric values (a multi-bucket-aggregation). This aggregation query allows sub-aggregations.

Extends `AggregationQueryParams`.

| Property | Type | Description |
|----------|------|-------------|
| `extendedBounds` | DoubleRangeValue | Sets optional extended bounds if parameter minDocCount equals to zero. Explanation: By default the histogram returns all the buckets within the range of the data itself, that is, the documents with … |
| `hardBounds` | DoubleRangeValue | The optional hard_bounds is a counterpart of extended_bounds and can limit the range of buckets in the histogram. It is particularly useful in the case of open data ranges that can result in a very … |
| `interval` | DoubleSingleValue | Specifies the width of generated buckets. The interval must be greater than zero. Important: The generated buckets include the lower bound and exclude the upper bound, i.e. [from, to). |
| `minDocCount` | int32 | If minDocCount = 0, the response will fill gaps in the histogram with empty buckets. It is possible to change that and request buckets with a higher minimum count by means of the min_doc_count … |
| `missingValue` | DoubleSingleValue | The optional missingValue parameter defines how documents that are missing a value should be treated. By default they will be ignored but it is also possible to treat them as if they have a value. |
| `offset` | DoubleSingleValue | Provides an optional offset to change the start value of each bucket by the specified positive offset. For example, when using an interval of 10, buckets will run from [0, 10), [10, 20), [20, 30) and … |
| `order` | AggregationOrderE | The optional order buckets are retrieved. By default, the histogram aggregation orders buckets by their key ascending. If this value is omitted, the default will be used. |
| `subAggregations` | MapToAggregationQuery | Inner aggregations of this aggregation. |

## `HistogramAggregationResult`

The result object of a histogram aggregation on numeric values. It consists mainly of buckets and within its inner aggregations results (sub-aggregations).

| Property | Type | Description |
|----------|------|-------------|
| `buckets` | ListOfBucket | The result buckets. |

## `IXExceptionC`

This class contains constant definitions for Indexserver error numbers used in Indexserver exceptions. Copyright: Copyright (c) 2004 Organisation: ELO Digital Office GmbH

| Property | Type | Description |
|----------|------|-------------|
| `ACCESS_DENIED` | int32 | Access to the specified object was denied, there are rights missing. |
| `ALREADY_EXISTS` | int32 | Specified object to be created already exists. |
| `AUTHENTICATION_FAILED` | int32 | User authentication failed |
| `FAILED_TO_DELETE_PACKAGE` | int32 | EIX-2040: Specified package can not be deleted. |
| `FAILED_TO_DELETE_PACKAGE_LEVEL` | int32 | EIX-2647: Specified package level can not be deleted. |
| `INTERNAL` | int32 | Internal error. |
| `INVALID_CRYPT_KEY` | int32 | Encryption key must be set before documents can be encrypted. |
| `INVALID_LICENSE` | int32 | License key is invalid. |
| `INVALID_PARAM` | int32 | Invalid parameter was given. |
| `INVALID_SESSION` | int32 | Session is not (or no longer) valid. |
| `LICENSE_LIMITATION` | int32 | EIX-3418: Error related to the limits imposed by the license. |
| `LOCKED` | int32 | Specified data is locked. |
| `NOT_EMPTY` | int32 | Specified data could not be found. |
| `NOT_FOUND` | int32 | Specified data could not be found. |
| `NOT_IX` | int32 | Not a ELO IX Exception |
| `PASSWORD_DENIED` | int32 | The given password violates the password rules. The passord rules are stored in the user profile value `UserProfileC#KEY_PASSWORD_RULES`. |
| `PROHIBITED_OPERATION` | int32 | The called function cannot be executed on this object |
| `SCRIPT_ERROR` | int32 | An error thrown in a script file is passed to the client application with this error code. E.g. [ELOIX:6000] "Message text thrown in script". |
| `SERVER_ERROR` | int32 | Problem in the server configuration. |
| `SYNTAX_ERROR` | int32 | Syntax error e.g. in search request. |
| `TEMP_PROBLEM` | int32 | Temporarily problem, a later try should succeed. |
| `TEMP_PROBLEM_OLD` | int32 | Temporarily problem, a later try should succeed. The value of TEMP_PROBLEM must be changed in IX 8.00.054, because the current client libraries recognize it as an exception where relogin is required. … |
| `TIMEOUT` | int32 | Request timed out. EIX-3140 |
| `UNABLE_TO_TAKE_OVER_ASPECT_OBJECTS` | int32 | Can not take over aspect objects. |
| `UNSUPPORTED_FUNCTION` | int32 | Function is not currently supported. |
| `UNSUPPORTED_FUNCTION_SINCE` | int32 | Since version x, the function is no longer supported. |
| `UNSUPPORTED_PROTOCOL_VERSION` | int32 | Unsupported protocol version. This error is thrown, if the protocol version cannot be negotiated. A reason for this can be different major release versions of client and server. |

## `IXExceptionData`

This class describes an exception that can occur during the execution of an Index server function. Copyright: Copyright (c) 2004 Organisation: ELO Digital Office GmbH

| Property | Type | Description |
|----------|------|-------------|
| `callId` | string | String containing the ClientInfo.callId for the ClientInfo object. This is given to the function which triggered/caused the error. |
| `details` | string | Detail text for the exception. |
| `exceptionType` | int32 | This is the type of exception. This is one of the constants from IXExceptionC. |
| `message` | string | The error message text for the exception. |
| `ticket` | string | Ticket given to the function. |

## `IXServerEventsContext`

Execution context of server events. An object of this class is passed to every server event and registered function. On the server side, it can be casted to IObjectFactory to retrieve helper objects for access checking, conversion of date values and numeric values, etc.

| `clientComputer` | string | Parameter clientComputer from login function. |
| `sessionOptions` | MapToString | SessionOptions associated to this connection. |
| `url` | string | Indexserver URL. |
| `user` | UserInfo | Current user |

This class represents a condition to filter aspect data of type `IndexValueC#TYPE_DOUBLE` in the database.

## `IndexSearchOperator`

The constants in this class define the supported operations for conditions when searching for aspect objects in the database.

| `operand` | string | Value on the right side of the operation. The left side implicitly is the value in the database. |
| `operator` | IndexSearchOperator | Operator for this search condition |

## `IndexSearchValue`

This class is used to search for index values in aspect objects. Regardless of whether the search-mode is set to `SearchModeC#OR`, all of the conditions defined in this class must be met. Please not that you must use the correct field according to the aspect's line type. The Indexserver will not evaluate other fields. That means that if the aspect's line type is `AspectLineC#TYPE_DOUBLE`, the Indexserver will look at `#doubleValue` and `#doubleValues` only.

| Property | Type | Description |
|----------|------|-------------|
| `doubleValue` | IndexSearchDoubleValue | A single `IndexSearchDoubleValue`. Set this field if you only have a single condition for this line. This field is for convenience only. The Indexserver will merge its value into `#doubleValues`. … |
| `doubleValues` | ListOfIndexSearchDoubleValue | `List` of conditions that all must be fulfilled when searching for double values in aspect objects. The Indexserver will evaluate this field only if the respective aspect line is of type … |
| `fixedPointNumberValue` | IndexSearchFixedPointNumberValue | A single `IndexSearchFixedPointNumberValue`. Set this field if you only have a single condition for this line. This field is for convenience only. The Indexserver will merge its value into … |
| `fixedPointNumberValues` | ListOfIndexSearchFixedPointNumberValue | `List` of conditions that all must be fulfilled when searching for fixedPointNumber values in aspect objects. The Indexserver will evaluate this field only if the respective aspect line is of type … |
| `intValue` | IndexSearchIntegerValue | A single `IndexSearchIntegerValue`. Set this field if you only have a single condition for this line. This field is for convenience only. The Indexserver will merge its value into `#intValues`. … |
| `intValues` | ListOfIndexSearchIntegerValue | `List` of conditions that all must be fulfilled when searching for integer values in aspect objects. The Indexserver will evaluate this field only if the respective aspect line is of type … |
| `stringValue` | IndexSearchStringValue | A single `IndexSearchStringValue`. Set this field if you only have a single condition for this line. This field is for convenience only. The Indexserver will merge its value into `#stringValues`. … |
| `stringValues` | ListOfIndexSearchStringValue | `List` of conditions that all must be fulfilled when searching for string values in aspect objects. The Indexserver will evaluate this field only if the respective aspect line is not of type … |

## `InvalidateCacheC`

Constants for cache invalidation. This constans can be used as bit combination in function invalidateCache.

| Property | Type | Description |
|----------|------|-------------|
| `ALL` | int32 | Flag used to specify that all cached objects are invalid. |
| `ASPECTS` | int32 | Flag used to specify that the cached Aspect objects are invalid. |
| `CRYPT_INFO` | int32 | Flag used to specify that the cached crypt keys are invalid. |
| `DOCUMENT` | int32 | Flag used to specify that a cached document (script) has changed. |
| `HASHTAGCOUNT` | int32 | Flag used to specify that the hashtag count cache is invalid. |
| `ICONS` | int32 | Invalidate cache of icons. |
| `INDEXFIELD_DISPLAY_DATA` | int32 | Flag used to specify that the display data of references in index fields of type relation have changed. |
| `KEYS` | int32 | Flag used to specify that the cached keys are invalid. |
| `KEYWORD_LISTS` | int32 | Notify about modified keyword lists. |
| `MARKERS` | int32 | Flag used to specify that the cached ColorData objects are invalid. |
| `MASKS` | int32 | Flag used to specify that the cached DocMask objects are invalid. |
| `NOTE_AND_COLD_IMAGES` | int32 | Flag used to specify that the note and cold background images are invalid. |
| `NO_FORWARD_TO_OTHER_IXS` | int32 | Do not invalidate the caches of the other servers. |
| `OBJKEY_DISPLAY_DATA` | int32 | Flag used to specify that the display data of references in ObjKeys have changed. Note: there is a newer name for this constant flag: INDEXFIELD_DISPLAY_DATA. Please use the newer version see below. |
| `PATHS` | int32 | Flag used to specify that the cached Path objects are invalid. |
| `REPL_NAMES` | int32 | Flag used to specify that the cached ReplName objects are invalid. |
| `SESSION` | int32 | Notify about modified session options or permissions. Reserved for internal usage. |
| `SORD_TYPES` | int32 | Flag used to specify that the cached SordType objects are invalid. |
| `SUBSCRIPTIONS` | int32 | Flag used to specify that the subscription cache is invalid. |
| `TRANSLATE_TERMS` | int32 | Flag used to specify that the translate terms are invalid. |
| `USERS` | int32 | Flag used to specify that the cached users are invalid. |
| `WORKFLOWS` | int32 | Flag used to specify that the workflow cache is invalid. |
| `WORKSPACE_ACLS` | int32 | Invalidate cache of workspace/teamspace ACLs. |

## `KeywordsDynamicInfo`

This Class contains parameters for the IX call `IXServicePortIF#checkoutKeywordsDynamic(ClientInfo, KeywordsDynamicInfo)` . There are two ways to execute the script for dynamic keyword lists. According to the parameter in this class, the one way or the other is chosen. The first way is to specify `KeywordsDynamicInfo#sord` and `KeywordsDynamicInfo#maskLineFocus`. Then, the IX passes these parameters to the script via the script-function open(ec, sord, fieldName). The other parameters will be overwritten by `null` and completely ignored. The other way is to set the fields `KeywordsDynamicInfo#mapData`, `KeywordsDynamicInfo#mapLineFocus` and `KeywordsDynamicInfo#mapScriptName`. In this case, the IX passes these parameters to the scripting function openMap(ec, data, fieldName). To achieve this, `sord` as well as `maskLineFocus` must be `null`.

| Property | Type | Description |
|----------|------|-------------|
| `aspectAssocFocus` | AspectAssoc | Only for sords with data organization based on aspects. At the client, set this value to the aspect association being currently in focus. This value determines (together with field aspectLineFocus) … |
| `aspectLineFocus` | AspectLine | Only for sords with data organization based on aspects. At the client, set this value to the aspect line being currently in focus. This value determines (together with field aspectAssocFocus) the … |
| `mapData` | MapToString | Represents the input data of a form. The script to execute uses the values in this map to filter the resulting data set. |
| `mapLineFocus` | string | Represents the id of a field in a form. |
| `mapScriptName` | string | Determines the script to execute. This value has only an effect if sord and maskLineFocus are set to null. Setting this value requires to set the values mapLineFocus and mapData also. |
| `maskLineFocus` | DocMaskLine | Only for sords with data organization based on objkeys. At the client, set this value to the mask line that currently has the focus. This value determines the server script to be executed. The server … |
| `ordinalFocus` | int32 | Only for sords with data organization based on aspects. If aspectAssocFocus has a cardinality of `Cardinality#MANDATORY_MANY` or `Cardinality#OPTIONAL_MANY`, this value can optionally be given to set … |
| `sord` | Sord | This value contains data gathered by the client. The contents of sord will be given to the script to execute. |

| `table` | ListOfListOfString | This value contains the result set as a table. Each entry of the outer list represents a row in the table. Thus, the entry i of an inner list represents the value of the i . column of the actual row. … |
| `title` | string | A brief and succinctly description about the represented data. The script developer must provide a title, otherwise an exception is thrown. |

## `LockC`

Constants to lock data against concurrent modification.

| Property | Type | Description |
|----------|------|-------------|
| `DOC` | LockZ | Lock or unlock document versions. Works like `LockC#YES` but is only valid at check*Sord() and check*Doc() respectively. |
| `FORCE` | LockZ | Lock or unlock regardless of whether the data is locked by another user. This requires main administration privileges in most cases. |
| `IF_FREE` | LockZ | A lock is only set, if the record is unlocked. A lock request is refused too, if the record is already a lock by the current user. |
| `NO` | LockZ | Do not lock the requested record(s). |
| `SORD` | LockZ | Lock or unlock the indexing information. Works like `LockC#YES` but is only valid at check*Sord() and check*Doc() respectively. |
| `YES` | LockZ | Lock or unlock. The lock is set, if the record is unlocked or locked by the current user. With respect to locking the indexing information (LockC.SORD) and document versions (LockC.DOC), LockC.YES … |
| `bsetDOC` | string |  |
| `bsetFORCE` | string |  |
| `bsetIF_FREE` | string |  |
| `bsetNO` | string |  |
| `bsetSORD` | string |  |
| `bsetYES` | string |  |

| Property | Type | Description |
|----------|------|-------------|

| `clientInfo` | ClientInfo | ClientInfo object returned by the IX when the login is carried out. Contains information about the client settings. |
| `ticketLifetime` | int32 | Contains the lifetime of the ticket in seconds. The connection is terminated once this lifetime has expired. |
| `user` | UserInfo | Contains the data detailling the user used to login to the IX. |

## `MapData`

This class represents a map which contains user defined key-value pairs.

| Property | Type | Description |
|----------|------|-------------|
| `TStamp` | string | Timestamp |
| `TStampSync` | string | EIX-3097 TimestampSync |
| `domainName` | string | Map domain name. An arbitary name or one of the predefined names in MapDomainC. |
| `guid` | string | GUID |
| `id` | string | Map ID. If the map belongs to a Sord ID, this value is equal to the member objId. |
| `items` | KeyValue[] |  |
| `lockId` | int32 | User ID of the user that owns the lock on the map. If the map is not locked, this value is -1. |
| `lockName` | string | User name of the user that owns the lock no the map. If the map is not locked, this value is an empty string. |
| `mapItems` | MapToMapValue | Map items represented by class MapValue which gives access to BLOB values. |
| `objId` | int32 | Object ID. The ID of the associated Sord object or 0, if the map does not belong to a Sord object. |

Constant class for MapData

| Property | Type | Description |
|----------|------|-------------|
| `lnBlob` | int32 | Maximum length of blob value. 1048576 Bytes |
| `lnId` | int32 | Maximum length of map id. |
| `lnKey` | int32 | Maximum length of map item key. |
| `lnValue` | int32 | Maximum length of map item value. |

## `MapDomain`

This class contains the information of a map domain. A map is a set of key value pairs and can be addressed by a map domain name and a map ID. All maps with the same domain name are stored in the same database tables. These maps are distinguished by their ID, which can be an arbitary string. A map can be attached to a Sord object by setting the map ID to the Sord ID. Attached maps are deleted, when the Sord object is finally deleted. Furthermore they can be copied with the Sord object.

| Property | Type | Description |
|----------|------|-------------|
| `copy` | boolean | This value is true, if the map items should be copied when the associated Sord object is copied. |
| `history` | boolean | This value is true, if a history should be maintained for map items. A history is always available for `MapDomainC#DOMAIN_SORD` and `MapDomainC#DOMAIN_WORKFLOW_ACTIVE`. |
| `name` | string | Map domain name. This can be a user definined name or one of the predefined names in MapDomainC. The value is internally used as part of a database table name. Thus only alphanumeric characters are … |
| `replicate` | boolean | This value is true, if the map items should be replicated with the associated Sord object. |

## `NavigationInfo`

NavigationInfo is used as traversal information for structured bulk operations restricting specific processsing, e.g. pooled jobs, to scalable amounts. It provides parameters controlling the traversal. Copyright: Copyright (c) 2004 Organisation: ELO Digital Office GmbH

| Property | Type | Description |
|----------|------|-------------|
| `findInfo` | FindInfo | Specify additional filters for the tree walk. Please note that specifying a custom findInfo always leads to a second search for each directory decreasing the overall performance. Use this only if … |
| `ignoreDocuments` | boolean | ignore the navigation of documents |
| `maxCount` | int32 | maxCount is the overall maximum amount of visited nodes and is independent of successful processing. maxCount does not depend on successful processing to be incremented, and it can stop the traversal … |
| `maxDepth` | int32 | The maximum depth of the tree walk. Passing maxDepth < 1 turns off this limit. |
| `maxSiblings` | int32 | The maximum number of siblings for one tree level. This limit is most useful for ignoring the contents of large folders. Passing maxSiblings < 1 will turn the limit off. |
| `startIDs` | string[] |  |

## `NavigationInfoC`

Constants class for the NavigationInfo class. Copyright: Copyright (c) 2004 Organisation: ELO Digital Office GmbH

| Property | Type | Description |
|----------|------|-------------|
| `MIN_NAV_DEPTH` | int32 | The minimum depth for tree walks. |
| `MIN_NAV_MAX_COUNT` | int32 | The overall limit for any visited element. |
| `MIN_NAV_SIBLINGS` | int32 | The minimum amount of collected siblings. |
| `SKIP_NAV_LIMIT` | int32 | This constant is used to override the navigation limit. |

## `NotOperator`

This operator contains a single QueryObject which is excluded from the search. This means, a document is returned if the QueryObject is not present in the document.

Extends `QueryOperator`.

| Property | Type | Description |
|----------|------|-------------|
| `queryFilter` | QueryFilter | A negated QueryObject. |

## `Note`

This helper class inherits all members from `NoteData` and adds a static member to access the bit constants for the `NoteData` members.

| Property | Type | Description |
|----------|------|-------------|
| `TStamp` | string | Timestamp of the last change to the note. The format is JJJJ.MM.DD.hh.mm. ss |
| `TStampSync` | string | Timestamp of this object's last export by the replication. |
| `XPos` | int32 | The position in the x axis when the note is displayed on a document in ELO. Taken from the top left corner of the document. For NoteC.TYPE_ANNOTATION_FREEHAND and … |
| `YPos` | int32 | The position in the Y axis when the note is displayed on a document in ELO. Taken from the top left corner of the document. |
| `access` | int32 | Access rights for the current user. Read-only. |
| `acl` | string | Access control list. Only used for notes of type TYPE_ANNOTATION_MARKER. Set member aclItems=null on check in otherwise it is ignored. |
| `aclItems` | AclItem[] |  |
| `color` | int32 | RGB value. Undefined for notes of type TYPE_ANNOTATION_STAMP and TYPE_ANNOTATION_NOTE_WITHFONT. |
| `createDateIso` | string | ISO encoded external (user defined) date. |
| `deleteDateIso` | string | The Note is deleted at this date. ClientInfo determines the Timezone. Is undefined if isDeleted() returns false. |
| `deleted` | boolean | True if the note is deleted, otherwise false. |
| `desc` | string | The text for the note. |
| `guid` | string | The GUID for the note. |
| `height` | int32 | The height of the note (y axis), when displayed on a document in ELO. |
| `id` | int32 | The unique id for the note. |
| `lockId` | int32 | The ID of the user that holds the lock or -1, if the note is not locked. |
| `lockName` | string | The user name that holds the lock or an empty string if the note is not locked. |
| `noteFreehand` | NoteFreehand | Point information for a freehand line. Valid only for notes of type TYPE_ANNOTATION_FREEHAND and TYPE_ANNOTATION_HORIZONTAL_LINE. Either desc, noteText, noteImage or noteFreehand can be set. |
| `noteImage` | NoteImage | Additional information for image stamps. Valid only for notes of type TYPE_ANNOTATION_STAMP. Either desc, noteText, noteImage or noteFreehand can be set. |
| `noteText` | NoteText | Additional information for textual stamps. Valid only for notes of type TYPE_ANNOTATION_NOTE, TYPE_ANNOTATION_STAMP and TYPE_ANNOTATION_NOTE_WITHFONT. Either desc, noteText, noteImage or noteFreehand … |
| `objId` | string | Object ID of the associated Sord object or any of the ID specifiers that are valid for checkoutSord too. Functions createNote and checkoutNotes return only numerical object IDs. |
| `ownerId` | int32 | The ID of the user who created the note. Administrators can set the ownerId to an arbitary user ID in checkinNotes. Set ownerName="" in this case. |
| `ownerName` | string | The name of the user that has created the note. Administrators can set the ownerName to an arbitary user name in checkinNotes. Set ownerId=-1 in this case. |
| `pageNo` | int32 | The page number to which the note is attached. |
| `type` | int32 | The note type. |
| `width` | int32 | Width ( x axis) of the note when displayed on a document in ELO. |

## `OcrInfo`

This class defines the properties of an OCR request.

| Property | Type | Description |
|----------|------|-------------|
| `busId` | string | Return OCR result over this event bus. If the OCR request should be performed asynchronously, this value must contain the event bus ID on which the result event is sent. If this value is 0, the OCR … |
| `eventId` | string | OCR result should be sent in an event with this event ID. This member helps to map the OCR request to the OCR result. Optional. |
| `messagesLanguage` | int32 | Language of error messages produced by the OCR engine. One of the OcrInfoC. MESSAGES_LANGUAGE_ constants. Optional. |
| `queryLanguages` | OcrInfoQueryLanguages | This member is set in order to query the languages supported by the OCR engine. Optional. |
| `recognizeFile` | OcrInfoRecognizeFile | Recognize file information. Set this member in order to process OCR on a file or an archive document. Optional. |

## `OcrInfoC`

Constants for OCR processing.

| Property | Type | Description |
|----------|------|-------------|
| `ALL_PAGES` | int32 | OCR will process all pages of the given document. |
| `CHAR_AND_RECT` | int32 | Compute character positions. The OCR result is a binary stream of subsequent records of this layout. Character record UTF-16 Character 2 Bytes Horizontal position of upper left corner 4 Bytes … |
| `CHAR_AND_RECT_EX` | int32 | Compute character positions. The OCR result is a binary stream as defined in `#CHAR_AND_RECT` prefixed by a header of this layout: Header Magic 4 Bytes (Integer) Version 4 Bytes (Integer) Skew Angle … |
| `CHAR_AND_RECT_XML` | int32 | Compute character positions and return XML format. |
| `ENCODING_UTF16` | int32 |  |
| `ENCODING_UTF16LE` | int32 |  |
| `ENCODING_UTF8` | int32 |  |
| `EVEN_PAGES` | int32 | Internal Use only. OCR will process all even pages of the given document. |
| `MESSAGES_LANGUAGE_BULGARIAN` | int32 |  |
| `MESSAGES_LANGUAGE_CZECH` | int32 |  |
| `MESSAGES_LANGUAGE_DUTCHSTANDARD` | int32 |  |
| `MESSAGES_LANGUAGE_ENGLISH` | int32 |  |
| `MESSAGES_LANGUAGE_ESTONIAN` | int32 |  |
| `MESSAGES_LANGUAGE_FRENCH` | int32 |  |
| `MESSAGES_LANGUAGE_GERMAN` | int32 |  |
| `MESSAGES_LANGUAGE_HUNGARIAN` | int32 |  |
| `MESSAGES_LANGUAGE_ITALIAN` | int32 |  |
| `MESSAGES_LANGUAGE_LATVIAN` | int32 |  |
| `MESSAGES_LANGUAGE_LITHUANIAN` | int32 |  |
| `MESSAGES_LANGUAGE_POLISH` | int32 |  |
| `MESSAGES_LANGUAGE_PORTUGUESE` | int32 |  |
| `MESSAGES_LANGUAGE_RUSSIAN` | int32 |  |
| `MESSAGES_LANGUAGE_SLOVAK` | int32 |  |
| `MESSAGES_LANGUAGE_SPANISH` | int32 |  |
| `MESSAGES_LANGUAGE_UKRAINIAN` | int32 |  |
| `ODD_PAGES` | int32 | Internal Use only. OCR will process all odd pages of the given document. |
| `PDF` | int32 | PDF output. OCR will convert the output in a PDF format. |
| `PDF_COMPLIANCE_NONE` | int32 | Compliance with PDF/A standard is not necessary. |
| `PDF_COMPLIANCE_PDFA_1A` | int32 | The recognized text should be exported to PDF/A-1a format. |
| `PDF_COMPLIANCE_PDFA_1B` | int32 | The recognized text should be exported to PDF/A-1b format. |
| `PDF_COMPLIANCE_PDFA_2A` | int32 | The recognized text should be exported to PDF/A-2a format. |
| `PDF_COMPLIANCE_PDFA_2U` | int32 | The recognized text should be exported to PDF/A-2u format. |
| `PDF_COMPLIANCE_PDFA_3A` | int32 | The recognized text should be exported to PDF/A-3a format. |
| `PDF_COMPLIANCE_PDFA_3U` | int32 | The recognized text should be exported to PDF/A-3u format. |
| `PDF_QUALITY_BALANCED` | int32 | The PDF (PDF/A) export will be balanced between the quality of the resulting file, its size and the time of processing. |
| `PDF_QUALITY_MAXQUALITY` | int32 | Optimize the PDF (PDF/A) export in order to receive the best quality of the resulting file. |
| `PDF_QUALITY_MAXSPEED` | int32 | Optimize the PDF (PDF/A) export in order to receive the highest speed of processing. |
| `PDF_QUALITY_MINSIZE` | int32 | Optimize the PDF (PDF/A) export in order to receive the minimum size of the resulting file. |
| `SPACES_EXACT` | int32 | Used in SingleCoulmnMode. The OCR tries to insert the correct number of blanks. |
| `SPACES_NORMAL` | int32 | Used in SingleCoulmnMode. All words are separated by one blank. |
| `SPACES_TWOSPACES` | int32 | Used in SingleCoulmnMode. If the words are separated by a larger distance, a second blank is inserted. |
| `TEXT` | int32 | Text output. The OCR result is a UTF-16LE character stream. |
| `UNIT_INCH` | int32 |  |
| `UNIT_MILLIMETER` | int32 |  |
| `UNIT_PER_THOUSAND` | int32 |  |
| `UNIT_POINT` | int32 |  |
| `WHITESPACE_DEFAULT` | int32 | Let the OCR Whitespace characters as they are. |
| `WHITESPACE_REPLACE` | int32 | OCR Whitespace characters will be replaced: Line break and Paragraph break -> '\n' Tabulation -> '\t' Object replacement character -> ' ' Soft hyphen -> '-' |
| `XML` | int32 | XML output. The OCR result is a xml style layout. |

## `OcrInfoRecognizeFile`

This class describes an OCR analysis request.

| Property | Type | Description |
|----------|------|-------------|
| `accuracy` | int32 | Recognition accuracy. A value of 0 activates the fast mode, a value of 1 activates the exact mode. |
| `encoding` | int32 | OcrInfoC. ENCODING_UTF8 / _UTF16 / _UTF16LE |
| `imageData` | FileData | Image file content. Either imageData or objId must be set. |
| `minCharConfidence` | int32 | Minimum confidence of character recognition in percent. Characters that are recognized with a lower confidence are replaced by `#replaceChar`. Optional. |
| `objId` | string | Object ID of an archived document to be analyzed. Either imageData or objId must be set. |
| `originalFileName` | string | Original Filename. allows tracking through the log files of the different modules. |
| `outputFormat` | int32 | Output format. Must be one of the following: `OcrInfoC#TEXT`, `OcrInfoC#CHAR_AND_RECT`, `OcrInfoC#CHAR_AND_RECT_EX` `OcrInfoC#PDF`, `OcrInfoC#XML` |
| `pageNo` | int32 | Page number. The first page number is 0. If all pages should be analyzed, set pageNo = -1. |
| `pageNumbers` | int32[] |  |
| `pageTimeout` | int32 | Recognition timeout for a single page. Cancel recognition if it least longer than this number of seconds. Optional. |
| `pdfCompliance` | int32 | OcrInfoC. COMPLIANCE_PDFA_3U / _3A / _2U / _2A / _1B / _1A / _NONE |
| `pdfQuality` | int32 | OcrInfoC. PDF_QUALITY_MAXQUALITY / _BALANCED / _MINSIZE / _MAXSPEED |
| `recognizeLangs` | string[] |  |
| `recognizeRects` | OcrRect[] |  |
| `rectUnit` | int32 | Rectangle coordinates are based on this unit. Use on of the constants OcrInfoC.UNIT_*. Optional. |
| `replaceChar` | int32 | Replacement for characters. Characters that are recognized with a lower confidence than `#minCharConfidence` are replaced this character. Optional. |
| `singleColumnMode` | boolean | Disable table recognition. Set this member to true, if the OCR should not try to recognized columns and rows of tables. Optional. |
| `spaces` | int32 | OcrInfoC. NORMAL/TWOSPACES/EXACT |
| `timeoutSeconds` | int32 | Recognition timeout. Cancel recognition if it least longer than this number of seconds. Optional. |
| `whitespace` | int32 | OCR returns whitespace characters. Must be one of the following: `OcrInfoC#WHITESPACE_DEFAULT`, `OcrInfoC#WHITESPACE_REPLACE` |

| `exception` | string | Error message. Null or empty, if no error has occurred. This value is used only in asynchronous processing in order to inform the client application, that an error has occured. In synchronous … |
| `exceptionID` | int32 | Error id. 0, if no error has occurred |
| `queryLanguages` | OcrResultQueryLanguages | Supported languages. This member is returned, if `OcrInfo#queryLanguages` was set. |

## `OcrResultRecognizeFile`

This class contains the result of an OCR analysis.

| Property | Type | Description |
|----------|------|-------------|
| `height` | int32 | Page height. Only valid if a single page was analyzed, see `OcrInfoRecognizeFile#pageNo`. |
| `skewAngle` | double | Skew angle. Only valid if a single page was analyzed, see `OcrInfoRecognizeFile#pageNo`. |
| `text` | string | Recognized text. This member is set, if `OcrInfoRecognizeFile#outputFormat` was set to `OcrInfoC#TEXT`. |
| `textData` | FileData | Binary data. This member is set, if `OcrInfoRecognizeFile#outputFormat` was set to `OcrInfoC#CHAR_AND_RECT`. |
| `width` | int32 | Page width. Only valid if a single page was analyzed, see `OcrInfoRecognizeFile#pageNo`. |

This operator contains a list of QueryObjects and concatenates them with an "OR" operation. This means, a document is returned if at least one of the QueryObjects is present in the document. For REST calls, use class QueryOperator and set the operator type in `QueryOperator#operatorType`.

|----------|------|-------------|

## `PhysDel`

This class represents one row in the database table physdel. This table contains the guids of physically deleted objects. Those objects can be any objects with a guid: e.g. folders, documents, versions, users, workflows...

| Property | Type | Description |
|----------|------|-------------|
| `TStamp` | string | The object is deleted at this timestamp. Measured in the UTC time-zone. |
| `TStampSync` | string | Timestamp of this object's last export by the replication. |
| `guid` | string | GUID of deleted object. |
| `type` | int32 | Type of deleted object. |

| `parameters` | MapToString | String parameters. |
| `source` | PluginMessageSource | Message source. Read only. |
| `status` | int32 | Message response status code. In case of a HTTP request, this element specifies the response status code. A value of 0 is implicitly mapped to 200. |

## `PreviewImageInfo`

| Property | Type | Description |
|----------|------|-------------|
| `documentId` | string | Id of the document(-version) to query preview images for. If set, `objectId` is ignored. |
| `endPage` | int32 | Last page number of the document to process a preview image for. If this value is ≤ 0, all pages of the document ≥ startPage are process. If 0 < endPage ≤ startPage holds, only the startPage will be … |
| `objectId` | string | ID of the objects to query preview images for. The ID must point to a document. Otherwise, an exception is thrown. |
| `previewSize` | int32 | Desired size of the preview images. Valid values are defined in `PreviewImageInfoC`. |
| `processDocument` | boolean | If processDocument is true, not only the URLs for each page are generated but also preview images are processed for the pages (startPage+1) to endPage in the background. |
| `renderAnnotations` | boolean | Enables rendering of annotations. If `renderAnnotations` is true , the preview images will also contain the annotations. If the preview images should contain the annotations but not the preview of … |
| `renderAnnotationsData` | boolean | Does only has an effect, if `renderAnnotations` is true . If set to true there will be details rendered near the annotations. Details are the annotation's author and create date. |
| `renderAnnotationsOnly` | boolean | Does only has an effect, if `renderAnnotations` is true . If set to true the annotations will be rendered but the rendering of document pages will be omitted. |
| `startPage` | int32 | First page number of the document to process a preview image for. The number of the first page of a document is 1. If this value is ≤ 0, 1 is assumed. |

## `PreviewImageInfoC`

| Property | Type | Description |
|----------|------|-------------|
| `DEFAULT_DPI` | int32 | Default dots per inch for scalable vector graphics. |
| `SIZE_LARGE` | int32 | Flag that signalizes the server to scale down preview images to a size suitable for most browser's canvas element. Choosing this settings will scale down only very large images. |
| `SIZE_MEDIUM` | int32 | Flag that signalizes the server to process preview images in a medium size. Images of this size are optimized in memory usage. They lack readability of text smaller than 10 points. |
| `SIZE_ORIGINAL` | int32 | Flag that signalizes the server to process preview images in the size of the original document. In case scalable documents sources like PDF, `PreviewImageInfoC#DEFAULT_DPI` is used. |
| `SIZE_TINIER` | int32 | Flag that signalizes the server to process preview images in a size for tiny thumbnails. The maximum length of an edge will be 100 pixels. |
| `SIZE_TINY` | int32 | Flag that signalizes the server to process preview images in a size for thumbnails. |

## `PreviewImageResult`

| Property | Type | Description |
|----------|------|-------------|
| `dimensionHeight` | int32 | Height of the first page. Please consider the hints at `PreviewImageResult#dimensionWidth` with respect to multi-page documents. |
| `dimensionWidth` | int32 | Width of first page. Use this value to get an idea about the dimensions of the preview image in general. While this value is exact for the first page, following pages may have different dimension … |
| `dpi` | int32 | Dots per inch of the requested preview images. If the document source is a multi-page file, this value holds for every requested pages. |
| `pages` | int32 | Number of pages. |
| `progress` | int32 | Current progress of processing at the server. Processing is finished at server side when this value equals `PreviewImageResult#progressMax`. |
| `progressMax` | int32 | Estimated value about the workload at the server. There is a chance that the server is finished with the processing of the request when progress reaches this estimated value. In repeated requests … |
| `statusMessage` | string | Status message about the current processing of preview images at the server. |
| `urls` | ListOfString | List of URLs pointing to the requested preview images. The URL at the first index in the list points to the lexicographic ordered first requested page. |

## `ProcessInfo`

Specific processing information for each node of processTrees(...) or processFindResults(...). The operations will be for existence (not null) in order of their appearance in ProcessInfo. Some of the underlying structures may allow toggling between prefix and postfix processing when used with processTrees. Copyright: Copyright (c) 2004 Organisation: ELO Digital Office GmbH

| Property | Type | Description |
|----------|------|-------------|
| `delStatus` | int32 | pass 0 for valid (undeleted) nodes, >0 otherwise. |
| `desc` | string | Holds the user defined description of a specific call. This member must not be null or empty. |
| `errorMode` | int32 | From ProcessInfoC: ERRORMODE_ALL, ERRORMODE_SKIP_SUBTREE, ERRORMODE_SKIP_PROCINFO or ERRORMODE_CRITICAL_ONLY. |
| `forceOperation` | boolean | Defines, if the data allways have to be written |
| `ignoreDocuments` | boolean | processing flag for documents. |
| `ignoreStructures` | boolean | processing flag for structure elements. |
| `inclReferences` | boolean | Process references (logical copies) too. |
| `lockZ` | LockZ | Use this parameter to access potentially locked nodes. |
| `procAcl` | ProcessAcl | Prefix processing, valid ProcessAcl data. |
| `procCopyElements` | ProcessCopyElements | Copy the elements into other archive location. |
| `procCountElem` | ProcessCountElements | Prefix processing count all elements |
| `procFulltext` | ProcessFulltext | Prefix processing, valid ProcessFulltext data. |
| `procInheritKeywording` | ProcessInheritKeywording | Inherit keywording. |
| `procMoveDocumentsToStoragePath` | ProcessMoveDocumentsToStoragePath | Move documents to storage path. |
| `procMsgMax` | int32 | Maximum amount of recorded errors in procMsgs; from ProcessInfoC: PROCMSGMAX. |
| `procMsgs` | string[] |  |
| `procRegion` | ProcessRegion | Process region of sub-trees. |
| `procReleaseLock` | ProcessReleaseLock | Release the locks on processed elements. |
| `procReplSet` | ProcessReplSet | Prefix processing, valid ProcessReplSet data. |
| `procScript` | ProcessScript | Either prefix or postfix processing. |
| `procSpaceGuid` | ProcessSpaceGuid | Workspace GUID processing. |

## `ProcessInfoC`

Constants class for the ProcessInfo class. Errors: The error modes are ordered by increasing failure tolerance. In most cases a tree walk will traverse nodes in prefix mode except scripts, that may have an user defined processing position, so the operational success of a given node directly influences further processing. ERRORMODE_ALL, the zero failure tolerance, stops the job (nearly) immediately, while ERRORMODE_SKIP_SUBTREE just skips subtree traversals, but continues with lists or siblings. If you pass ERRORMODE_SKIP_PROCINFO the sequence of ProcessInfo members will be executed completely, whether errors occur or not. The most tolerant mode is ERRORMODE_SKIP_PROCINFO where only errors impeding further traversing stop the job. Copyright: Copyright (c) 2004 Organisation: ELO Digital Office GmbH

| Property | Type | Description |
|----------|------|-------------|
| `DELSTATUS_ALL` | int32 | Do not filter by SORD status. |
| `DELSTATUS_DELETED` | int32 | filter ony deleted SORDs. |
| `DELSTATUS_VALID` | int32 | Filter only undeleted SORDs. |
| `ERRORMODE_ALL` | int32 | Processing will be stopped when an error occurs, regardless of the error type. |
| `ERRORMODE_CRITICAL_ONLY` | int32 | Stop only on non-recoverable errors. |
| `ERRORMODE_SKIP_PROCINFO` | int32 | Stop the ProcessInfo sequence for the current Node. |
| `ERRORMODE_SKIP_SUBTREE` | int32 | Stop the subtree processing (processTrees only), but continue with list elements respectively siblings. |
| `PROCMSGMAX` | int32 | Maximum allowed amount of error messages. |

## `QueryFilter`

A QueryFilter is used to describe a value to search for in a document. It references a field in which should be searched and its value. There are other usecases, in which a QueryFilter is used to describe a certain field and its value is not needed and therefore ignored: To retrieve context terms and completions. Usage: Define where to search for the value by the parameters searchField and indexFieldKey. If the field you want to search in is defined in SearchFieldC, use one of the constants. In this case, the value of indexFieldKey is ignored. If you want to search in an index field, set searchField = SearchFieldC.INDEXFIELD and provide its name in indexFieldKey. For a DocMask with data organization "Aspect" the indexFieldKey must contain the name of the aspectAssoc and the lineKey of the aspectLine separated by "¶". For a DocMask with data organization "ObjKey" or "Table" the indexFieldKey must contain the lineKey of the docMaskLine. Examples: Search for "Hello World" in the title: `fieldType = SearchFieldC.TITLE; FilterValue = new StringValue("Hello World"); fieldType = FieldTypeC.tokenized;` Search for "200" in a numeric index line called NUMERICFIELD: `fieldType = SearchFieldC.INDEXFIELD; docMaskLine="NUMERICFIELD", FilterValue = new IntValue(200); fieldType = FieldTypeC.numeric;`

Extends `QueryObject`.

| Property | Type | Description |
|----------|------|-------------|
| `boost` | float | Boost for calculated relevance. A field can be boosted and therefore has a higher influence on the calculated relevance. For more information, refere to … |
| `docMaskLine` | string | Provide the name of the index field here. If searchField != SearchFieldC.INDEXFIELD, this value is ignored. |
| `fieldType` | FieldTypeE | Define how to search for a value. Options are tokenized, not tokenized, numeric, date. The delivered FilterValue must match this fieldType, if not, an exception is thrown. |
| `indexFieldKey` | string | Provide the name of the index field here. If searchField != SearchFieldC.INDEXFIELD, this value is ignored. If the associated docmask is of type DocMaskC.DATA_ORGANISATION_OBJKEYS or … |
| `searchField` | SearchFieldE | Provide the constant of the field to search in. To search in a index field, set this to SearchFieldC.INDEXFIELD and provide its name in indexFieldKey. |
| `value` | FilterValue | The value for the QueryFilter. |

| `startEventId` | int32 | If the value of startEventId is ≥0, only eventIds ≥ that value will be returned. Optional. |

## `QueryObject`

A QueryObject is used to build a search query. It can either be a QueryOperator, which is a either a list of concatenated QueryObjects or a negated QueryObject, or a QueryFilter which describes an actual value of a document.

## `QueryOperator`

A QueryOperator is used to include or exclude QueryObjects from a search and to concatenate QueryObjects. Like this, complex queries can be build.

|----------|------|-------------|

| Property | Type | Description |

| `lockId` | int32 | ID of user who has locked the reminder record in DB |

| `notifyOnDelete` | boolean | Notify sender if receiver deletes the reminder. |
| `notifyOnView` | boolean | Notify sender if receiver views the reminder. |

| `objGuid` | string | Sord GUID. |
| `objId` | int32 | Reminder is linked to this Sord. |
| `objType` | int32 | Sord type. |
| `prio` | int32 | Priority: 0...high, 1...medium, 2... low |
| `promptDateIso` | string | At this Date the reminder should be made visible to the receiver. |
| `receiverDisplayName` | string | Locale specific name of this reminder's receiver. Read-only. This value is the resolved `UserInfo#nameTranslationKey`. |
| `receiverId` | int32 | Reminder was created for this user. |
| `receiverName` | string | Reminder was created for this user. |
| `senderDisplayName` | string | Locale specific name of this reminder's sender. Read-only. This value is the resolved `UserInfo#nameTranslationKey`. |
| `senderId` | int32 | Reminder was created by this user. |
| `senderName` | string | Reminder was created by this user. |

| `extra3` | string | Reserved. |

| `id` | string | Identifier |
| `objId` | int32 | Id of the object for which the action is entered in the protocoll. |
| `sessionNo` | int32 | Id of the session which carried out the action. |
| `text` | string | Reserved - internal, dependant on actionNo |
| `userId` | int32 | Id of the user who carried out the action. |
| `userName` | string | Name of the user who carried out the action. |

## `ReportInfoC`

Report information. Indexserver writes the following report entries: Report Entries ReportInfo.action ReportInfo.objId ReportInfo.extra1 ReportInfo.extra2 ReportInfo.comment ACT_IX_LOGIN 0 SSO User ID [EXTRA2_SSO_LOGIN] Computer name ACT_IX_LOGOUT 0 0 0 ACT_IX_LOCK_ARCHIVE 0 Key ID 0 Key name ACT_IX_CREATE_USER 0 User ID User Flags User name ACT_IX_CHECKIN_USER 0 User ID User Flags User name ACT_IX_DELETE_USER 0 User ID User Flags User name ACT_IX_CREATE_KEY 0 Key ID 0 Key name ACT_IX_CHECKIN_KEY 0 Key ID 0 Key name ACT_IX_DELETE_KEY 0 Key ID 0 Key name ACT_IX_CREATE_SORD Object ID Object type Storage mask ID Sord name ACT_IX_CHECKOUT_SORD Object ID 0 combination of EXTRA2_* Sord name ACT_IX_CHECKIN_SORD Object ID 0 combination of EXTRA2_* Sord name ACT_IX_DELETE_SORD Object ID Object type combination of EXTRA2_* Sord name ACT_IX_DELETE_REFERENCE Object ID Parent ID combination of EXTRA2_* Sord name ACT_IX_COPY_SORD unsupported ACT_IX_REFERENCE_SORD Parent ID Object ID Parent name, Object name ACT_IX_MOVE_SORD New parent ID Object ID Old parent ID New parent name, old parent name, Sord name ACT_IX_LINK_SORD From object ID To object ID From Sord name, To Sord name ACT_IX_UNLINK_SORD From object ID To object ID From Sord name, To Sord name ACT_IX_CHECKOUT_DOCVERSION Object ID Document version ID combination of EXTRA2_* Objektname, Versionsnummer ACT_IX_CHECKIN_DOCVERSION Objekt-ID Doc-ID combination of EXTRA2_* Objektname, Versionsnummer ACT_IX_CREATE_DOC_MASK 0 Mask-ID 0 Maskname ACT_IX_CHECKIN_DOC_MASK 0 Mask-ID 0 Maskname ACT_IX_DELETE_DOC_MASK 0 Mask-ID replace with Mask-ID Maskname ACT_IX_CHECKIN_REPL_NAME 0 Repl-ID 0 ReplSet-Name ACT_IX_CHECKIN_REPORT_OPTIONS 0 0 0 ACT_IX_DELETE_REPORT 0 endDate 0 TS-End-Date ACT_IX_CHECKIN_COLOR 0 Color-ID rgb Color-Name ACT_IX_CHECKIN_USER_PROFILE 0 For User-ID 0 User-Name ACT_IX_DELETE_USER_PROFILE 0 For User-ID 0 User-Name ACT_IX_CREATE_WORKFLOW 0 Workflow-ID combination of EXTRA2_* Workflow-Name ACT_IX_CHECKIN_WORKFLOW 0 Workflow-ID combination of EXTRA2_* Workflow-Name ACT_IX_DELETE_WORKFLOW 0 Workflow-ID combination of EXTRA2_* Workflow name ACT_IX_EDIT_WORKFLOW_NODE Object-ID Workflow-ID Node-ID Node name ACT_IX_START_WORKFLOW Object-ID Workflow-ID Template-WF-ID Workflow name ACT_IX_START_ADHOC_WORKFLOW Object-ID Workflow-ID 0 Workflow name ACT_IX_TERMINATE_WORKFLOW Object-ID Counter-Name ACT_IX_INCREMENT_COUNTER 0 Value 0 Counter name ACT_IX_CHECKIN_SORD_TYPE 0 ID (Sord.type) 0 Type name ACT_IX_DELETE_SORD_TYPE 0 ID (Sord.type) 0 Type name ACT_IX_CREATE_NOTE Objekt-ID Note-ID (internal) combination of EXTRA2_* Note-Guid ACT_IX_CHECKIN_NOTE Objekt-ID Note-ID (internal) combination of EXTRA2_* Note-Guid ACT_IX_DELETE_NOTE Objekt-ID Note-ID (internal) combination of EXTRA2_* Note-Guid ACT_DM_READDOC Objekt-ID Doc-ID 0 ACT_IX_SUBSTITUTION_NEW 0 `Substitution#userId` `Substitution#substituteId` ACT_IX_SUBSTITUTION_CHANGE 0 `Substitution#userId` `Substitution#substituteId` ACT_IX_SUBSTITUTION_DELETE 0 `Substitution#userId` `Substitution#substituteId` ACT_IX_SUBSTITUTION_ACTIVATE 0 `Substitution#userId` `Substitution#substituteId` ACT_IX_SUBSTITUTION_DEACTIVATE 0 `Substitution#userId` `Substitution#substituteId` ACT_IX_SUBSTITUTION_TRANSFER 0 `Substitution#userId` `Substitution#substituteId` previous substituteId ACT_IX_SUBSTITUTION_FORWARD 0 `Substitution#userId` `Substitution#substituteId` previous substituteId To enable or disable reporting of actions, action codes have to be transformed into ERP codes first. One ERP code can subsume serverel action codes. The ERP codes can be used in checkinReportOptions to manipulate reporting. Furthermore they can be used in findFirstReportInfos, FindReportInfo, to search for reported actions. The following table shows how actions codes are mapped to report options. | `userInfoEff` | UserInfo | UserInfo object with effective permissions. |

## `ResolveRightsInfo`

Parameter Class for `IXServicePortIF#resolveRights(ClientInfo, UserInfo, ResolveRightsInfo)` .

## `ResolveRightsResult`

Class for the result of the IX-Function `IXServicePortIF#resolveRights(ClientInfo, UserInfo, ResolveRightsInfo)` .

| Property | Type | Description |
|----------|------|-------------|
| `members` | ListOfString | Path of members that explains why the user got this right. The first item in this list is the name of the object directly assigned to the user. The last item is the name of the object which obtains … |
| `right` | int32 | The right the user got by `ResolveRightsResult#type`. Values will be of `AccessC`.FLAG_*. |
| `right2` | int32 | The right the user got by `ResolveRightsResult#type`. Values will be of `AccessC`.FLAG2_*. |
| `type` | int32 | The kind of way the user got this right. See `ResolveRightsResultC` for valid values. |

## `ResolveRightsResultC`

Constants for `ResolveRightsResult`.

| Property | Type | Description |
|----------|------|-------------|
| `DIRECT` | int32 | Indicates that the user got the right directly. |
| `INHERITED` | int32 | Indicates that the user got the right by inheritance of a group. |
| `SUBSTITUTION` | int32 | Indicates that the user got the right by a substitution rule. |

## `RestoreOptions`

This class contains several options for undeleting archive SORDs by `restoreSord` Copyright: Copyright (c) 2004-2006 Organisation: ELO Digital Office GmbH

| Property | Type | Description |
|----------|------|-------------|
| `minDeleteDate` | string | Restore only document versions that are deleted after `minDeleteDate`. Its String representation is the ISO format in the client's time zone. |
| `minIDate` | string | `minIDate` ist the ISO representation of the local date used to mark the lower limit for restoring. |
| `parentId` | string | Since references also got a delete date, they can be restored independently from their main reference. To restore a reference selectively, set parentId to the references' parentId. Restoring a … |
| `singleObject` | boolean | `singleObject=true` restricts the operation to the specified object not traversing sub trees. |

## `SearchFieldE`

Use this class of constants to define in which field should be searched or aggregated. For every option, allowed `FieldTypeE` is given which is also the fallback type if provided type is incorrect.

Values: `INDEXFIELD`, `OBJ_ID`, `GUID`, `TITLE`, `FULLTEXT`, `EXTRA_TEXT`, `OWNER_ID`, `OWNER_NAME`, `FILE_EXTENSION`, `FILENAME`, `TIMESTAMP`, `I_DATE`, `X_DATE`, `DELETED_DATE`, `DOCUMENT_SIZE`, `MASK_ID`, `MASK_NAME`, `TYPE`, `PARENT_GUID`, `VERSION_NUMBER`, `VERSION_COMMENT`, `VERSION_OWNER_ID`, `FEED_TEXT`, `FEED_AUTHOR`, `FEED_HASHTAG`, `FEED_MENTION`, `FEED_REFERENCE`, `FEED_TYPE`, `FEED_DATE_CREATED`, `FEED_DATE_UPDATED`, `SORD_HASH`, `SPACE_GUIDS`, `REGION_ID`, `EXTERNAL_ID`, `REFERENCE_ID`, `SORD_TYPE_DESIGNATION`, `CONFIDENTIALITY`

## `SingleValueMetricsAggregationQueryParams`

Defines parameters to run an aggregation of one of the types Min, Max, Sum, Avg (single-value-metrics-aggregations). This aggregation query doesn't allow sub-aggregations. Note: The value_count aggregation gets an an own query params class although it is a single-value-metrics-aggregation, because it has a different return value type (long).

Extends `AggregationQueryParams`.

| Property | Type | Description |
|----------|------|-------------|
| `missingValue` | string | The missing parameter defines how documents that are missing a value should be treated. By default they will be ignored but it is also possible to treat them as if they had a value. Note: At ELO this … |

## `SingleValueMetricsAggregationResult`

The result object of a single-value-metrics aggregation. It consists mainly of a result value.

| Property | Type | Description |
|----------|------|-------------|
| `value` | double | The result of the metrics aggregation. |

## `SordDetails`

Objects of this class are data elements and control the values assigned to certain boolean properties(yes/no attributes).

| Property | Type | Description |
|----------|------|-------------|
| `arcReplEnabled` | boolean | True, if replication is enabled for this archive. |
| `archivingMode` | int32 | Archiving mode. The possible values are version controlled ArchivingModeC.VERSION read-only ArchivingModeC.READONLY read/write ArchivingModeC.READWRITE This value is only valid for document objects. |
| `documentContainer` | boolean | Folders can be marked as a document container by this flag. If true, clients are advised to preview the first document in this folder on selection. |
| `encryptionSet` | int32 | Encryption set number. |
| `fulltext` | boolean | Document is or should be containted in the fultext database. |
| `fulltextDone` | boolean | Document is indexed in the fultext database. |
| `incomplete` | boolean | True, if a document or attachment version has been deleted. |
| `inheritAclDisabled` | boolean | Do not inherit permissions from parent Sord. |
| `isDynamicFolder` | boolean | Is true iff the current sord is a dynamic folder. |
| `limitedReleaseDocument` | boolean | Documents using this mask may be used as release notes |
| `linked` | boolean | True, if this object has links to other Sords. This member is read-only and is ignored in checkinSord and checkinDocEnd. |
| `linkedPermanent` | boolean | True, if this object has permanent links to other Sords. This member is read-only and is ignored in checkinSord and checkinDocEnd. |
| `region` | boolean | If this sord is a folder, it establishes a region within the repository tree. This flag is only valid for sords with a mask of data organisation `DocMaskC#DATA_ORGANISATION_ASPECT`. The region … |
| `replRoot` | boolean | True, if this object is a root folder for replication. |
| `sortOrder` | int32 | Sort order of child entries. This value is only valid for folder objects. |
| `translateSordName` | boolean | If `true` translate the sord's short description into or from the user language. |
| `type` | SordTypeEnum | The type of sord object. |
| `workspace` | boolean | If `true` the sord is a workspace folder. |

## `SordHist`

Version history for the keywording from an object. A version history is created for a sord object when the keywording is changed for the first time (a newly created sord has no version history). Once created only one SordHist object exist for the sord object and is assigned to the sord object via the Sord.guid = SordHist.objGuid relationship. The keywording properties that were altered in the change process are saved in an array of SordHistKey objects. Copyright: Copyright (c) 2004 Organisation: ELO Digital Office GmbH

| Property | Type | Description |
|----------|------|-------------|
| `aspectObjects` | MapToListOfMapToIndexValue | Index values. This member is only valid, if the associated keywording form is organized as `DocMask#dataOrganisation` = `DocMaskC#DATA_ORGANISATION_ASPECT`. . |
| `histGuid` | string | GUID of the SordHist object. |
| `histKeys` | SordHistKey[] |  |
| `histSource` | int32 | Identifies the application with which changes were made to the keywording. |
| `objGuid` | string | GUID of the sord object. |
| `replServerId` | string | The name of the replication server from which this change for this sord was made. Corresponds to `ServerInfo#replProcessOnServerId` |
| `timeStampLocal` | string | Timestamp of the change in the timezone of the client. The format is JJJJ.MM.DD.hh.mm. ss |
| `timeStampUTC` | string | Timestamp of the change in the timezone of the client, in UTC form. The format is JJJJ.MM.DD.hh.mm.ss |
| `userName` | string | The name of the user who carried out the changes. |
| `userNo` | int32 | The user who carried out the changes. |
| `workStation` | string | The name of the computer from which the change was carried out. This is the parameter clientComputer which is provided by the ix.login function. |

## `SordType`

Each file structure element is assigned a SordType. This SordType has three icons , which are used in the different view in the client: a standard icon, a disabled icon (empty folders or references) and a workflow icon. The icons are available in BMP, ICO and JPEG format. Copyright: Copyright (c) 2004 Organisation: ELO Digital Office GmbH see@ SordTypeC SordTypeC

| Property | Type | Description |
|----------|------|-------------|

| `icon` | FileData | File data of normal icon. |
| `id` | int32 | Sord type ID. |
| `name` | string | Type name. |
| `nameTranslationKey` | string | Translation-keyword for `SordType#name`. |
| `packageName` | string | Package name of SordType |
| `type` | SordTypeEnum | Type of SordType |
| `workflowIcon` | FileData | File data of workflow icon. |

| Property | Type | Description |

| `IALPHA` | int32 | Subitems are sorted by name descending. |
| `ID` | int32 | Used for ascending ordering of users by id. This value can be used for `FindUserInfo#sortOrder` |
| `IDATE` | int32 | Subitems are sorted by internal date. |
| `IEXTERNALID` | int32 | Used for descending ordering of Sords by externalId. Can only be used for searches. |

| `IIDATE` | int32 | Subitems are sorted by internal date descending. |

| `IREFERENCEID` | int32 | Used for descending ordering of Sords by referenceId. Can only be used for searches. |
| `IUSERNAME` | int32 | Used for the descending ordering of threads by username. This value can be used for `FindBackgroundThreadOptions#sortOrder`. |
| `IXDATE` | int32 | Subitems are sorted by external date descending. |
| `MANUAL` | int32 | Subitems are sorted manually. |

| `NONE` | int32 |  |
| `REFERENCEID` | int32 | Used for ascending ordering of Sords by referenceId. Can only be used for searches. |
| `USERNAME` | int32 | Used for the ascending ordering of threads by username. This value can be used for `FindBackgroundThreadOptions#sortOrder`. |
| `XDATE` | int32 | Subitems are sorted by external date. |
| `dummy` | int32 |  |

## `StartAdHocWorkflowInfo`

This class contains several options that are used to start the AdHocWorkflow

| Property | Type | Description |
|----------|------|-------------|
| `acceptMessage` | string | This message is shown if the workflow is accepted by a person. |
| `actionRejectMessage` | string | This message is shown if the workflow is rejected. |
| `breakWorkflowMessage` | string | This message is send to the user who started the workflow if the workflow is canceled. |
| `cancelMessage` | string | The message that is send to the user specified with `cancelUserId`, if the workflow is canceled. |
| `cancelUserId` | string | If the workflow is canceled, this user receives a message, `cancelMessage`. |
| `deactivateNodes` | boolean | If true, all person nodes are deactivated when the workflow is canceled. This option is only used for "parallel for validation" workflow. Otherwise this option is ignored. |
| `finishedMessage` | string | The message that is send to the user specified with `finishedUserId`, if the workflow is finished. |
| `finishedScript` | string | This script is executed, when the workflow is finished. |
| `finishedUserId` | string | If the workflow is finished, this user receives a message, `finishedMessage`. |
| `flags` | int32 | Control flags for the node, a combination of WFNode.C.FLAG_* constants. |
| `forValidation` | boolean | If true, the workflow comes back to the user who started it. |
| `nodeInfos` | ListOfValueClass | The information of all nodes in the workflow. |
| `nodeName` | string | Start node name. |
| `noticeMessage` | string | This message is shown if the a person was informed about the workflow. |
| `serialFlow` | boolean | If true, the workflow routes the object from user to user. The workflow finishes when the last user has edited their node. If `serialFlow` is false, the workflow routes the object to all users. The … |
| `successMessage` | string | This message is shown if the workflow is successfully processed. |
| `timeLimit` | WFTimeLimit | Time-limit for the entire workflow. |
| `timeLimitEscalations` | WFTimeLimit[] |  |
| `userIdsToDeactivate` | string[] |  |
| `workflowRepeatMessage` | string | This message is send to the user who started the workflow if the workflow is repeated. |

## `StoreInfo`

This class represents a storage path. Copyright: Copyright (c) 2004 Organisation: ELO Digital Office GmbH Usage for Bucket Storages (S3 and Azure) Amazon S3 For Amazon S3 the following attributes must be specified: name: any string scatter: 13 for normal path, 14 for backup path; see `de.elo.ix.storeext.ScatterInfoE` cloudType: 0 for MinIO; see `de.elo.ix.storeext.StorageTypeE` endpoint: URL to S3 account accessKey: from S3 account secretKey: from S3 account bucket: bucket name region: from S3 account encryptionkey: key to encrypt data before upload retentionMode: `NONE(0), GOVERNANCE(1), COMPLIANCE(2)`; see `de.elo.ix.storeext.RetentionMode` Azure Blob Storage The Azure Blob Storage mostly uses the same attributes like S3 (except region which doesn't exist in Azure). However, in Azure terminology, these attributes have different names. For sake of simplicity, we use the same variables that are also used for S3. name: any string scatter: 16 for normal path, 17 for backup path; see `de.elo.ix.storeext.ScatterInfoE` cloudType: 3; see `de.elo.ix.storeext.StorageTypeE` endpoint: URL to Azure storage account accessKey: accountName in Azure secretKey: accountKey in Azure bucket: container in Azure encryptionkey: key to encrypt data before upload retentionMode: `NONE(0), AZURE_IMMUTABLE_UNLOCKED(3), AZURE_IMMUTABLE_LOCKED(4)`; see `de.elo.ix.storeext.RetentionMode`

| Property | Type | Description |
|----------|------|-------------|
| `accessKey` | string | AccessKey for Cloud Storage. |
| `bucket` | string | Bucket name for Cloud Storage. Bucket names must be unique across all existing bucket names in Amazon S3 / Azure. |
| `cloudType` | int32 | Type of Cloud Storage. |
| `createDateISO` | string | Create date (UTC). |
| `dir` | string | Physical directory where the documents of this path are stored. Length of the field in database: 255byte. |
| `encryptionKey` | string | Encryption key for Cloud Storage. |
| `endpoint` | string | Endpoint (URL/IP/PORT) for Cloud Storage. |
| `flags` | int32 | Bitset of options. |
| `fulltextDir` | string | Physical directory where fulltext content is stored. Length of the field in database: 255byte. |
| `id` | int32 | Path ID Set this value to -1 in order to create a new storage path. |
| `name` | string | Path name. Length of the field in database: 16byte. |
| `previewDir` | string | Physical directory where previews stored. Length of the field in database: 255byte. |
| `region` | string | Region for Amazon S3 Cloud Storage. |
| `retentionMode` | int32 | RetentionMode for S3 Storage |
| `scatter` | int32 | Granularity of subdirectories in the physical directory. |
| `secretKey` | string | SecretKey for Cloud Storage. |
| `stdPath` | boolean | standard path? read-only |
| `updateDateISO` | string | Update date (UTC). |

## `StoreInfoC`

Definition of a document path. Copyright: Copyright (c) 2004 Organisation: ELO Digital Office GmbH

| Property | Type | Description |
|----------|------|-------------|
| `CLOUD_AZURE` | int32 | Azure Blob Storage. |
| `CLOUD_GOOGLE` | int32 | Google Cloud Storage. not yet implemented. |
| `CLOUD_MINIO` | int32 | Minio Cloud Storage. |
| `CLOUD_S3` | int32 | Amazon S3 Cloud Storage. |
| `DMPATH_ELOSYS` | string | Reserved path name. Documents stored within DMPATH_ELOSYS will not be purged. |
| `FLAG_BACKUP` | int32 | Backup path. |
| `FLAG_ELO5_NO_NT_SECURITY` | int32 | Only ELO 5.0: no NT security. |
| `FLAG_ELO5_PATH_CHANGED` | int32 | Only ELO 5.0: path has been changed. |
| `FLAG_ELO5_PATH_PROTECTED` | int32 | Only ELO 5.0: path is protected. |
| `FLAG_NO_RESTORE` | int32 | Used with FLAG_BACKUP, document files are not restored in a normal path. |
| `FLAG_READONLY` | int32 | Path is read-only. |
| `FLAG_WORM` | int32 | Path can only be written once. DM does not use temporary files. |

| `SP_AZURE` | int32 | Use Azure Blob Storage |
| `SP_AZURE_EXTERNAL` | int32 | Use Azure Blob Storage as backup device |
| `SP_CENTERA` | int32 | Store on CENTERA device. |
| `SP_FLAT` | int32 | No sub directories. |
| `SP_FULL` | int32 | 256 folders with 256 documents |
| `SP_MD5_HASH` | int32 | Store using MD5 hash. |
| `SP_REL_FILE_PATH` | int32 | External storage definition. A physical (relative) path is specified for each document. |
| `SP_S3` | int32 | Store on S3 Cloud Storage |
| `SP_S3_EXTERNAL` | int32 | Use S3 Cloud Storage as Backup Device |
| `SP_TSM` | int32 | Store on TSM device. |

| `subsName` | string | Deputy name. If this member is set `subsId` is ignored. |
| `userId` | int32 | The user that is being deputized by `subsId` |
| `userName` | string | User name. If this member is set `userId` is ignored. |

## `Subscription`

This class describes a subscription. A user can subscribe for changes to an object. Currently, the only supported object type is a document feed. If the feed receives new comments, notification information is inserted into the database for the user. By calling FeedService.findFirstActions and setting FindActionsInfo.findNotifications=true, the user can search for her notifications.

| Property | Type | Description |
|----------|------|-------------|
| `createDateIso` | string | Create date of the subscription. |
| `userGuid` | string | GUID of user who registered the subscription. |
| `watchGuid` | string | GUID of Object to be watched. This is either a `de.elo.ix.client.feed.Feed#getGuid()`, `de.elo.ix.client.feed.Action#getGuid()` or `de.elo.ix.client.feed.HashTag#getHstgGuid()`. |

## `Substitution`

A Substitution is used to create, update and read substitutions.

| Property | Type | Description |
|----------|------|-------------|
| `access` | int32 | The current users access rights for this Substitution. Returns a combination of AccessC.LUR_ constants. Read-only. |
| `creatorDisplayName` | string | Locale specific user name of the user that has created the Substitution Read-only. This value is the resolved `UserInfo#nameTranslationKey`. |
| `creatorId` | int32 | User who created the Substitution. Read-only. |
| `creatorName` | string | User who created the Substitution. Read-only. |
| `forwardedBy` | ListOfInteger | If the substitution was forwarded by `IXServicePortIF#forwardSubstitution`, the original substitutes (`#substituteId` are saved here. Read-only |
| `forwardedFrom` | string | If a substitution was forwarded to an other one, this references the `#guid` of the substitution it was forwarded from. Read-only. |
| `forwardedTo` | string | If a substitution was forwarded to an other one, this references the `#guid` of the substitution it was forwarded to. Read-only. |
| `groupsToInheritRights` | ListOfInteger | List of groups of which the substitute inherits the rights. It is not possible to inherit rights from a user, only from groups. Every group needs to have the the right … |
| `guid` | string | GUID of substitution A new substitution is saved if guid is empty on checkin. An existing substitution is updated if a guid is provided. |
| `lockDisplayName` | string | Locale specific user name of the user that has locked the Substitution If null, no lock is held. Read-only. This value is the resolved `UserInfo#nameTranslationKey`. |
| `lockId` | int32 | User ID of the user that has locked the Substitution If -1, no lock is held. Read-only, ignored in `IXServicePortIF#checkinSubstitutions` |
| `lockName` | string | Name of the user that has locked the Substitution Read-only, ignored in `IXServicePortIF#checkinSubstitutions` |
| `message` | string | Message to add to the substitution. |
| `settings` | SubstitutionSettings | Contains all boolean settings for a Substitution |
| `status` | SubstitutionStatus | Returns the status of the substitution. In case of checking in a substitution, this variable is ignored. |
| `substituteDisplayName` | string | Locale specific user name of the user or group to whom the substitution is directed. Read-only. This value is the resolved `UserInfo#nameTranslationKey`. |
| `substituteId` | int32 | User or group to whom the substitution is directed. This variable is ignored when calling `IXServicePortIF#checkinSubstitutions` if `#substituteName` is set. |
| `substituteName` | string | User or group to whom the substitution is directed. `#substituteId` is ignored if this member is set when calling `IXServicePortIF#checkinSubstitutions`. |
| `substituteType` | int32 | Type of the substitute. Read-only, refers to `UserInfoC#TYPE_GROUP` or `UserInfoC#TYPE_USER` |
| `substitutionPeriods` | ListOfSubstitutionPeriod | List of time periods in which the substitution is activated automatically if `SubstitutionSettings#activatePeriodsAutomatically` is set to true. |
| `tstamp` | string | Timestamp of last update. Read-only. |
| `tstampsync` | string | Timestamp of last update. Read-only. |
| `userDisplayName` | string | Locale specific user name of the user or group which is substituted by this object. Read-only. This value is the resolved by the property `UserInfo#nameTranslationKey`. |
| `userId` | int32 | The user or group which is substituted by this object. This variable is ignored when calling `IXServicePortIF#checkinSubstitutions` if `#userName` is set. |
| `userName` | string | The user or group which is substituted by this object. `#userId` is ignored if this member is set when calling `IXServicePortIF#checkinSubstitutions`. |

## `SubstitutionPeriod`

A substitution period is used to define time periods for substitutions. A substitution is automatically activated during a time period if `SubstitutionSettings#activatePeriodsAutomatically` is set to true. A time period always needs a start date, this date must not be in the past. The earliest start date is the current day to start a substitution immediately. The end date can be set, then the last day of the substitution is the end date (inclusive). It can also be left empty to have an open end for a substitution. A active substitution based on a time period can only be disabled by deleting the corresponding SubstitutionPeriod.

| Property | Type | Description |
|----------|------|-------------|
| `endIsodate` | string | End date of a substitution period (inclusive). If this value if empty, the substitution never ends. Date format must be either 'yyyyMMdd' or 'yyyyMMddHHmmss'. If only a date ('yyyyMMdd') is provided, … |
| `startIsodate` | string | Start date of a substitution period (inclusive) A start date must always be set. Date format must be either 'yyyyMMdd' or 'yyyyMMddHHmmss'. If only a date ('yyyyMMdd') is provided, it is adjusted to … |
| `substitutionGuid` | string | GUID of the corresponding `Substitution`. Read-only. |

## `SubstitutionSettings`

This class wraps various boolean settings and parameters for `Substitution`

| Property | Type | Description |
|----------|------|-------------|
| `activatePeriodsAutomatically` | boolean | Define if substitution is activated automatically during a `SubstitutionPeriod`. |
| `canAccessPersonalTasks` | boolean | Define if the substitute is allowed to find and access tasks (e.g. `WFDiagram`, `Activity`, `Reminder`) which are assigned to the user (`Substitution#userId` himself and not a substituted group … |
| `canAccessPostbox` | boolean | Define if the substitute can access the user's postbox. |
| `canBeActivatedManually` | boolean | Define if the substitution can be activated manually. |
| `canBeForwarded` | boolean | Define if the the substitution can be forwarded to an other user. |
| `inheritEffectivePrincipalRights` | boolean | If set to true, the principals effective rights (with all groups and so on) are inherited to the substitute. The substitute then is able to do everything the principal could. |
| `isActivatedManually` | boolean | Is the substitution activated manually by calling `IXServicePortIF#activateSubstitution(de.elo.ix.client.ClientInfo, String, de.elo.ix.client.LockZ)` Read-only |
| `superiorSubstitution` | boolean | Defines if a substitution was created as a superior substitution with all rights set. This flag cannot be set by a user himself, but the superior and administrators. To create such a substitution, … |

## `SubstitutionStatus`

The substitution status is used to inform if a substitution is currently deactivated or activated. If activated, it also informs how it was activated (either by a `SubstitutionPeriod` or manually)

Values: `DEACTIVATED`, `ACTIVATED_BY_PERIOD`, `ACTIVATED_MANUALLY`

## `UpDownloadEventInfo`

This class describes a document stream (version, attachment, preview, fulltext, signature) being uploaded or downloaded in an event of `DocumentEvents`.

| Property | Type | Description |
|----------|------|-------------|
| `docId` | int32 | Document version ID. Only valid for `DocumentEvents#beginDownload(de.elo.ix.client.IXServerEventsContext, UpDownloadEventInfo)`. |
| `kind` | UpDownloadKind | Kind of document related stream. |
| `length` | string | Number of bytes to download from a document related stream. Only valid for `DocumentEvents#beginDownload(de.elo.ix.client.IXServerEventsContext, UpDownloadEventInfo)`. If this element is -1, read up … |
| `offset` | string | Document related stream should be downloaded starting at this position. Only valid for `DocumentEvents#beginDownload(de.elo.ix.client.IXServerEventsContext, UpDownloadEventInfo)`. If the entire … |
| `preliminaryTest` | boolean | A preliminary test for a later uploaded stream. While uploading a content stream for a new document via `de.elo.ix.client.IXConnectionIF#upload(String, java.io.File)` to the server, the related Sord … |
| `sord` | Sord | Sord object. Check `Sord#getChangedMembers()` to find out, which members of the Sord object are valid. Usually, only `Sord#getId()` is set. This member is null, if the client application uses … |
| `usage` | UpDownloadUsage | From where an event is called. |

## `UpDownloadKind`

This enum defines constants to distinguish between different kinds of file data assigned to a document.

Values: `VERSION`, `ATTACHMENT`, `FULLTEXT`, `PREVIEW`, `SIGNATURE`

## `UpDownloadUsage`

This enumeration defines constants that describe, from where an event in `DocumentEvents` is called.

Values: `API`, `INTERN`

## `UrlParams`

This class describes additional params for an upload or download URL.

| `offset` | string | Download bytes beginning from this offset. |

## `UserProductClientType`

This enumeration defines the type of the client software the user is allowed to use. The definition of allowed features for each of the client types is subject to change and may vary between different versions of the software. For non-default values, the server will enforce that the user's client software suits the assigned `UserProductClientType`.

Values: `DEFAULT`, `FULL`, `INTEGRATION`, `WORKFLOW`, `RESEARCH`, `PORTAL`

| `groupLicenseKey` | string | Holds the group license key for groups requiring a license to be used. |

| `nodeId` | int32 | Node ID |
