## `WFNodeC`

Constants for `WorkFlowNode`.

| Property | Type | Description |
|----------|------|-------------|
| `DEACTIVATE_ALL_PREDS` | string | Indicates that all predecessor of the collect node should be terminated. |
| `FLAG_COPY_CHILDREN` | int32 | Node flag: This flag indicates that all nodes in the cycle should be copied when entering the begin cycle node. This flag is only used for the begin cycle node. |
| `FLAG_CYCLE_END` | int32 | Node flag: This flag denotes, whether a cycle node (TYPE_CYCLE) is the end of a cycle. |
| `FLAG_CYCLE_X` | int32 | Node flag: This is a copied start node of a cylce. Do not use this flag in workflow templates. The workflow engine will mark copied cycle. nodes with this flag. |
| `FLAG_DELEGATED` | int32 | Node flag: This flag indicates whether the person node was delegated. This flag is only used for the person node. |
| `FLAG_DO_NOT_START_MANUALLY` | int32 | Node flag: This flag indicates whether the sub workflow can be started manually. The sub workflow of. This flag is used for sub workflows. |
| `FLAG_ERROR` | int32 | Node flag: This flag indicates that this node is an error node, which is generated after an error occurred while executing the plugin node. |
| `FLAG_HIDDEN` | int32 | Node flag: Hide this workflow from default searches and listings. |
| `FLAG_NOT_VALID` | int32 | Node flag: Not released. A personal node of an Adhoc workflow has this flag set, if the user has not released the node. The Indexserver does not use this flag anywhere. |
| `FLAG_ONE_SUCCESSOR` | int32 | Node flag: Only one user can receive this node. |
| `FLAG_RESETADHOCNODE` | int32 | Node flag: Reset all person nodes. This flag is only used for parallel for validation workflow. |
| `FLAG_RESET_CHILDREN` | int32 | Node flag: Reset the ExitDate of the following nodes, if this node is activated. |
| `FLAG_ROOT_ADHOC` | int32 | Node flag: The start node of an Adhoc workflow has this flag. |
| `FLAG_TERMINATE_USER` | int32 | Node flag: End of workflow. This flag is set to the end node of an Adhoc workflow. The Indexserver does not need this flag but maybe the ELO Windows Client. |
| `FLAG_WORKINGDAYS` | int32 | Node flag: This flag indicates that weekend days should be skipped when evaluating time limits. |
| `TYPE_BEGINNODE` | int32 | Node type: Start of workflow. |
| `TYPE_CALL_SUB_WORKFLOW` | int32 | Note type: Call sub workflow. |
| `TYPE_COLLECTNODE` | int32 | Node type: Collects many workflow paths. |
| `TYPE_CYCLE` | int32 | Node type: Cycle Cycle nodes are contained in pairs in a workflow. One cycle node denotes the beginning of a cycle and an associated cycle node marks the end. Both cycle nodes must have the same name … |
| `TYPE_ENDNODE` | int32 | Node type: End of workflow. |
| `TYPE_IFNODE` | int32 | Node type: Decision node. |
| `TYPE_NOTHING` | int32 | Node type: undefined or deleted |
| `TYPE_PERSONNODE` | int32 | Node type: Personal node. A user must edit the node to continue the workflow. |
| `TYPE_PLUGINNODE` | int32 | Note type: Plugin node. This node is intended to trigger external events or to address external systems. OnEnter is handled synchronously. It waits for a result of the event. OnExit is handled … |
| `TYPE_SET_SERVER_ID` | int32 | Note type: Set the server ID where the WF can be continued. This node is used in replicated workflows. |
| `TYPE_SPLITNODE` | int32 | Node type: Distribute to many following nodes. |
| `USERID_IGNORE` | int32 | This value can be used in Node.department2 to make clear, that Node. department2 has to be ignored. |
| `USERID_OWNER` | int32 | Set WFNode. userId to this value, if a workflow node should belong to the user who starts the workflow. |
| `USERID_SUPERIOR` | int32 | This ID is a placeholder for the superior of the workflow owner. |

## `WFNodeMatrix`

Stores the relationship between workflow nodes Administers the bridges(connections) in a workflow diagram. These are objects of type WorkFlowNodeAssoc. Copyright: Copyright (c) 2004 Organisation: ELO Digital Office

| Property | Type | Description |
|----------|------|-------------|
| `assocs` | WFNodeAssoc[] |  |

## `WFNodeMatrixC`

These constants describe the type of connection between two nodes. Copyright: Copyright (c) 2004 Organisation: ELO Digital Office GmbH

| Property | Type | Description |
|----------|------|-------------|
| `ALWAYS` | int32 | Connection type for non-decision nodes. The connection will always be utilised to reach the next node. No conditions have to be fulfilled. |
| `IF_FALSE` | int32 | This connection is the FALSE connection following a decision node. This connection will be utilised when the IF requirement is not fulfilled. |
| `IF_OVERTIME` | int32 | This connection is only for the case of overtime. |
| `IF_TRUE` | int32 | This connection is the TRUE connection following a decision node. This connection will be utilised when the IF requirement is fulfilled. |

## `WFTimeLimit`

This class describes a time limit for a workflow or for a person node of a workflow.

| Property | Type | Description |
|----------|------|-------------|
| `overTimeLimit` | boolean | True, if the workflow exceeds the time limit. Read-only. |
| `timeLimit` | int32 | Time-limit in minutes. |
| `timeLimitIso` | string | After this date the node exceeds the time-limit for processing. Read-only. |
| `userId` | int32 | The ID of the user that should be informed, if the time-limit is exceeded. The Indexserver does not send any notification to the user. The client application is responsible for doing this. |
| `userName` | string | User name for timeLimitUserId; When writing a workflow with checkinWorkFlow, this value has preceedence before timeLimitUserId. Set timeLimitUserName to an empty string, if timeLimitUserId should be … |

## `WorkFlowActiveDoc`

Internal class.

| Property | Type | Description |
|----------|------|-------------|
| `activate` | int32 | ELO-date when the node was entered. DB column: wf_activate |
| `activeAcl` | string | Contains the ACL of the workflow. Only valid for begin nodes. |
| `alertFromBegin` | int32 | Time-limt for the entire workflow in minutes. This value is only valid for the begin node. DB column: wf_alert_from_begin |
| `alertTo` | int32 | ID of user who should be informed, if a time-limit exceeds. It might be the time-limit for the entire workflow or for a signle person node. DB column: wf_alert_to |
| `alertTo2` | int32 | ID of user who should be informed, if the time-limit alertWait2 exceeds. |
| `alertTo3` | int32 | ID of user who should be informed, if the time-limit alertWait2 exceeds. |
| `alertWait` | int32 | Time-limit for a single node. This value is only valid for person nodes. DB column: wf_alert_wait |
| `alertWait2` | int32 | Second Time-limit for a person node or begin node. |
| `alertWait3` | int32 | Third Time-limit for a person node or begin node. |
| `comment` | string | Comment, only valid for person nodes. DB column: wf_comment |
| `commentTranslationKey` | string | Translation-keyword for `WFNode#comment`. |
| `completionDate` | int32 | ELO-date when the workflow was finished. All nodes contain the same value. DB column: wf_completion_date |
| `condition` | string | Contains the ACL of the workflow template. Only valid for begin nodes. DB column: wf_condition |
| `delayDate` | int32 | Activate + DelayDays. Not valid for template workflows. Only valid for person nodes. DB column: wf_duedate |
| `delayDays` | int32 | An active person node (Activate is set) might be displayed to the user delayed by this number of days. Only valid for person nodes. DB column: wf_delaydays |
| `deleted` | int32 | Flag that indicates whether the workflow template is deleted. If the value is not 0, the workflow template is deleted. Only valid for template workflows. DB column: wf_deleted |
| `department` | int32 | ID of user who has to process the node. Might be a group ID too. Only valid for person nodes. DB column: wf_department |
| `department2` | int32 | A group ID to constrain access to the node. Only members of this group are allowed to see and process the node. Only valid for person nodes. DB column: wf_department2 |

| `designDepartment` | int32 | A group ID or user ID that was originally assigned in the designer. DB column: wf_designdepartment |

| `label` | string | Display name by forwarding. |
| `labelTranslationKey` | string | Translation-keyword for `WorkFlowActiveDoc#label`. |
| `lockId` | int32 | User ID of the owner of the lock. DB column: wf_locked_owner |
| `locked` | int32 | If node is locked, it contains 1 otherwise 0 DB column: wf_locked |
| `moveCyclePosX` | int32 | If a cycle is re-entered, the nodes in the cycle are duplicated. The copied nodes will be placed in the designer moved by this value to the right. If this value is 0, the nodes are moved 60 points … |
| `name` | string | Node name (work instruction). DB column: wf_name |

## `WorkFlowHead`

Helperclass to access the DB table workflowtemplhead, workflowactivedochead, ...

| Property | Type | Description |
|----------|------|-------------|
| `actServerId` | string | The current sever ID (resp. replication branch) where the workflow can be condinued. |
| `callNodeId` | int32 | The call node id of the main workflow, which call this sub workflow. |
| `flowId` | int32 | Workflow ID |
| `guid` | string | GUID |
| `parentWorkflow` | int32 | Id of the main workflow. |
| `status` | int32 | Status, != 0 means deleted |
| `tStamp` | string | Timestamp The format is JJJJ.MM.DD.hh.mm. ss |
| `tStampSync` | string | Timestamp of this object's last export by the replication. |
| `type` | int32 | Workflow type. |

## `WorkflowExchangeInfo`

This class represents a workflow export data.

| Property | Type | Description |
|----------|------|-------------|
| `subWorkflowMap` | MapToWFDiagram | Map contains the all direct und indirect subworkflows. The workflow ID is the key. |
| `subWorkflowVersionMap` | MapToArrayListOfWFDiagram | Map contains the workflow verions of the sub workflows. |
| `userNames` | MapToUserName | The users associated with the workflow |
| `workflow` | WFDiagram | The exchanged workflow. |
| `workflowVersions` | WFDiagram[] |  |

## `WorkflowExportOptions`

Structure for the options for the workflow-export. Copyright: Copyright (c) 2009 Organisation: ELO Digital Office GmbH

| Property | Type | Description |
|----------|------|-------------|
| `characterSet` | string | Character set for the export-data. |
| `flowId` | string | Id of the workflow, that will be exported. |
| `flowVersId` | string | Reserved. |
| `format` | int32 | Export format. |
| `wordWrap` | string | Word wrap for the export-data. |

## `WorkflowExportOptionsC`

Contants related to the workflow export.

| Property | Type | Description |
|----------|------|-------------|
| `FORMAT_JSON` | int32 | JSON format. |
| `FORMAT_PLAIN_TABLE` | int32 | Workflow table format for compatibility with Windows Client. |

## `WorkflowImportOptions`

Options for the workflow import.

| Property | Type | Description |
|----------|------|-------------|
| `collisionSuffix` | string | The content of this variable is extended to the name of the imported Workflow as a suffix, if the Workflow already exists inside ELO |
| `replaceMissingUserByUserId` | string | If this variable is set, the missing user is replaced by this user. Otherwise the missing user will be created using a random password. |

## `WorkflowNodeInfo`

| Property | Type | Description |
|----------|------|-------------|
| `flags` | int32 | Control flags for the node, a combination of WFNode.C.FLAG_* constants. |
| `nodeName` | string | The node name |
| `workflow` | string | The template ID of the subworkflow to start. |
